No build, flash or boot was performed; the device run needs a fresh explicit BOOT-only approval naming the CMD19 write, the data reset, the forced CMD12 and CMD13, the CMD14 read and the block-0 recovery read, and F3 remains \[U\].
- The 2026-11-04 protocol audit withdrew the byte-183 width-refusal, H12 DDR, stage-7 armed-timeout and H13 CMD14-CRC causal claims and fixed the underlying code. JESD84-B51 7.4 Table 93 defines BUS\_WIDTH\[183\] as W/E\_P, not readable, so a zero readback is not evidence of refusal; Linux mmc\_select\_bus\_width\(\) uses a bus test or read-only EXT\_CSD comparison. gta4l\_emmc\_set\_bus\_width\_verified\(\) now compares the read-only properties segment \(bytes 192..511\), restores the host and the card on failure and returns GTA4L\_EMMC\_ERR\_WIDTH\_RESTORE when the framing cannot be proven; the legacy byte-183 path remains only for already-flashed stages. H12 used reserved value 4, not DDR \(5/6\), and the helper is renamed set\_bus\_width\_reserved4. CMD14 bus-test reads tolerate one optional pattern-CRC indication \(JEDEC 6.6.4; Linux sdhci\_data\_irq\(\) exempts MMC\_BUS\_TEST\_R\) while normal reads keep CRC fatal. The 1-bit bus test uses the JEDEC 0x80 transmit pattern and the two-bit check. The stage-7 audit now writes TIMEOUT\_CONTROL 0x2E and reports toc/arm/restored, not CLOCK\_CONTROL bits. CMD18 checks live PRESENT\_STATE buffer readiness and acknowledges BRR after each sector; both simulators model readiness separately from the latched bit. H14 is paused because CMD12 is not appropriate for the single-block CMD19 write. H15 \(gta4l-emmc-probe=15\) is the corrected verified 4-bit SDR rung, host-tested only; no device run exists. Core 172/104 and probe 154/77; raw reports and images are preserved.
- The 2026-11-05 Stage-H15 cold boot passed the verified 4-bit SDR switch \(CMD6 status 0x00000900, HOST\_CONTROL 00-&gt;02, read-only EXT\_CSD properties matched, es=0x000009cb\) and then reproduced the CMD19 write-phase timeout at 4-bit \(rc=-11, err=0, nrm=0x11, PRESENT\_STATE 0x03f801f6\), so the wedge is not width-specific. The old H15 revert called the verified switch, whose pre-switch EXT\_CSD read was blocked by the latched DAT\_INHIBIT \(-17\), so no CMD6 was issued and the host stayed at 4-bit; the content pass and Q did not run, and the old report printed T att=1 and a default Q rc=0. The corrective batch adds explicit att flags and a T result code, gta4l\_emmc\_set\_bus\_width\_recover\(\) \(healthy-path baseline, bounded data reset before CMD6, read-only properties verification\), and the content-only H16 stage gta4l-emmc-probe=16 with no CMD19/CMD14. H15/H16 stop codes are 0/10/11/12/13/14/15/16. Core 176/104 and probe 158/77; no new image was built or flashed for this record, H14 remains paused and F3 remains \[U\].
- The 2026-11-05 Stage-H16 content-only device run returned rc=0 stop=0 on its first boot \(owner warm reboot from TWRP; BOOT-only image 4f1bfb90, payload 7404870a with a 7/7 contract, gate 18/18, commit 20c9f9a\). The verified 4-bit SDR switch passed on the first CMD6 attempt \(c6=00000900, hb=00 ha=02, vf=1, es=000009cb\), the full 4-bit content pass matched the 1-bit baseline \(T att=1 rc=0 same=1 div=0, b0=62084b66 b1=84d0543e data=f418cd33 last=a89b26d5\), the baseline-based width recovery worked on hardware \(V att=1 rc=0 c6at=1 c6=00000900 hb=02 ha=00 vf=1\) and the block-0 recovery read matched T b0 \(Q att=1 rc=0 qb0=62084b66\); B att=0 records that no CMD19/CMD14 was attempted, fail=0 and the PAD samples are unchanged. The W line read BUS\_WIDTH back as 0 where the H15 boot read 1, so byte 183 is not a usable switch indicator in either direction. 4-bit SDR sector reads are therefore measured \[C\] at 400 kHz and the write wedge is not a width-caused data-path failure. F3 remains \[U\]: only the CMD19/CMD14 round trip is outstanding, and the recorded write timeout is now read as the Linux/TI completion rule \(mmc\_bus\_test\(\) ignores BUS\_TEST\_W errors; TI SPRZ436H i836 expects the data timeout and proceeds to CMD14\) rather than a card or datapath verdict. Raw evidence stays outside the tracked tree at out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-probe-h16-evidence/ \(device-run-transcript.txt, device-run-record.json, flash-record.json\); docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.32.
- The 2026-11-05 Stage-H17 host batch applies the reference bus-test completion rule the recorded CMD19 wedge actually calls for. Linux mmc\_bus\_test\(\) discards every BUS\_TEST\_W error and lets BUS\_TEST\_R decide \(and returns 0 for a 1-bit bus\), sdhci\_data\_irq\(\) exempts MMC\_BUS\_TEST\_R from the data-CRC path and sdhci resets the data circuit on the data error, JEDEC 6.6.4 defines CMD19 as a pattern with no CRC status token, and TI erratum SPRZ436H i836 records the resulting controller data timeout with the guideline to clear it and continue to CMD14. The core gains an opt-in bus\_test\_tolerate\_write\_timeout flag plus write\_result/reset\_result fields: only a CMD19 DATA\_TIMEOUT is tolerated, the data circuit is cleared with one bounded local reset, and any other write error or a reset that cannot prove the circuit idle still stops. Stage 17 \(gta4l-emmc-probe=17\) runs the baseline, the verified 4-bit switch, the full 4-bit content pass, one CMD19 4-byte pattern write with the tolerated timeout, the data reset, one CMD14 read-back, the verified revert and the block-0 recovery read; it emits GTA4L-EMMC H17 REPORT with stop 0/10/11/12/13/14/15/16 and adds wrc/rst/ci to the shared H15/H16/H17 B line. Host checks: core 179/104 \(was 176/104\) with bus-test-tolerate-timeout, bus-test-tolerate-inhibit and bus-test-tolerate-write-crc, and probe 163/77 \(was 158/77\) with probe-h17-pass, probe-h17-content-div, probe-h17-pattern-mismatch, probe-h17-write-crc and probe-h17-inhibit; the H15 strict path keeps its recorded wrc=-11 trc=-11 behaviour. No build, flash or boot was performed; the device run needs a fresh explicit BOOT-only approval, and F3 remains \[U\] until the CMD19/CMD14 round trip is measured at 4-bit.
- The 2026-11-05 Stage-H17 device run closes the F3 bus-test clause at 4-bit. The a198db1 image \(boot image 13938e49, payload 72f6e2d4 with a 7/7 contract, gate 18/18\) was booted four times without reflashing \(cold, then three warm\); the first three stopped at the known pre-identification CMD3-completion timeout with no report, and the fourth \(warm\) returned rc=0 stop=0. The verified 4-bit SDR switch passed on the first CMD6 attempt \(c6=00000900, host 00-&gt;02, vf=1, es=000009cb\), the 4-bit content pass matched the 1-bit baseline \(same=1 div=0, b0=62084b66 b1=84d0543e data=f418cd33 last=a89b26d5\), and the CMD19 write produced the expected no-CRC-status timeout \(wrc=4294967285 = -11, fail record bound to CMD19 with CC latched, present 0x03f801f6\). The opt-in rule tolerated only that timeout, one bounded data reset cleared the circuit \(rst=0\), CMD14 returned the expected word \(trc=0, tw=te=000000a5\) - the first successful CMD19/CMD14 round trip on this device - and the verified revert \(02-&gt;00\), block-0 recovery read \(qb0=62084b66\) and unchanged PAD samples followed. fail=1 preserves the tolerated timeout by design; rc=0 stop=0 is the verdict. Byte 183 read back 1 here, 0 in H16 and 1 in H15, all on verified live 4-bit paths, so it is not a usable switch indicator in either direction. F3 is therefore measured PASS at 4-bit SDR, 400 kHz \[C\]; the 8-bit width is untried and remains open, and cold-boot identification reliability is a separate open issue. The report's panic location \(Gta41EmmcProbe.c:2775\) is the last argument line of the shared report panic in a198db1, binding the report to the flashed source. Two diagnostic carry-forwards are recorded for the next rung: last\_step is only advanced to BUS\_TEST after the CMD14 read, so the tolerated timeout's failure record reads step 18 \(PROBE\_LAST\); and the tolerated timeout leaves fail\_valid=1, so a later failure in the same boot would not be recorded. Raw report and decoded record are outside the tracked tree at out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-probe-h17-evidence/; docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.34.
- The 2026-11-05 Stage-C/F5 storage-publication design survey is recorded in docs/gta4l-storage-publication-design.md; it is host-only and adds no code, build, flash or boot. Key findings: the kernel prelink already carries com.apple.iokit.IOStorageFamily 2.1 with the IOBlockStorageDriver \(IOProviderClass IOBlockStorageDevice, device-type Generic\), IOGUIDPartitionScheme \(whole IOMedia, GUID\_partition\_scheme content mask\) and IOMediaBSDClient \(IOMedia, IOResourceMatch IOBSD\) personalities, and the APFS kext is prelinked; the public headers are prepared at build/storage-abi/include from revision e45fbe30, though the existing scripts/audit-storage-interface.py qualification artifact is not present in this checkout and must be produced before linking. NativeRamBlock and NativeVirtioBlock are working IOBlockStorageDevice templates \(complete override set, synchronous doAsyncReadWrite that completes the request, IOLock serialization, IOResources/IOBSD personality for the RAM transport\). The gta4l C core already exposes controller/card init and 512-byte CMD17 reads and has no public user-data write API; the probe maps SDHCI 0x04744000, GCC 0x01438000 and TLMM 0x00575000 through ml\_io\_map. The shared SM6115 repository has no SDHCI/block component and the catalog's only UFS driver is SM8550-specific, so the first publication driver belongs in the board repository. The proposed minimal graph is Gta4lEmmcBlock \(IOBlockStorageDevice, IOResources + IOBSD, guarded by a new XNU\_NATIVE\_GTA4L\_EMMC\_BLOCK personality\) -&gt; prelinked IOBlockStorageDriver -&gt; whole IOMedia -&gt; IOGUIDPartitionScheme partitions, with the personality added by a board KernelConfigTables patch and the personality flag plumbed through build-xnu-4k-baseline.sh like XNU\_NATIVE\_RAM\_BLOCK. The first rung would split the core into its own archive to avoid duplicate symbols, use a C request-loop helper plus a thin C++ adapter, boot with gta4l-emmc-block=1 \(report mode\), stay on 1-bit SDR at 400 kHz with per-block CMD17 reads, cap a request at 64 blocks, reject writes and report write protection, mark the media offline on the first read error rather than inventing an unproven reset, and have a bounded kernel thread report the whole media, the GPT partition media and one media-path read-back before panicking. No CMD6, CMD18/CMD12, CMD19, CMD25, user-data write, DMA, CQE, ICE, DDR, HS200/HS400, 8-bit change, mount or root switch is part of the first rung; the storage stack chooses arbitrary read-only LBAs, which is the main scope difference from the pinned probe list and needs explicit owner approval. F5 remains \[U\] because no IOMedia object has been observed.
- A 2026-11-05 source review corrected the Stage-C/F5 publication design before implementation. The existing storage-ABI qualification pipeline is not runnable as-is in this checkout: refer/IOStorageFamily is missing at the UniXNU root \(the gta4l tree has a symlink to the public feed at e45fbe30\), the Linux xcrun shim returns the literal macosx instead of a real SDK path, the audit hardcodes build/xnu-prelink/native-apfs-load-positive.macho, and the original candidate plan, the live storage metadata gdb-stop.json, the AES interface audit, build/storage-abi/interface-audit.json and build/ram-block/bindings.json are absent. The live observation is candidate-specific \(the audit requires gdb-stop.json:kernel\_sha256 == plan.candidate\_sha256; the RAM-root gdb-stop.json files are for dc8184da, not the gta4l candidate ca983f7d\), and the audit produces the concrete constructor/destructor/metaclass/method bindings, so a compile-only gate is not enough. The gta4l candidate, its plan with the storage extents and original\_executable\_sha256 852b7846, and the original kernelcache f18b4df8 are present. The qualification paths are \(1\) rebuild the original storage-audit harness on macOS and run it against the gta4l candidate to produce a gta4l gdb-stop.json, with the audit parameterized for --candidate/--plan, or \(2\) port the audit and observation harness to the Linux/QEMU setup and produce the gta4l observation there; do not link the driver until one succeeds. The corrected design also advertises kIOMaximumBlockCountReadKey/kIOMaximumByteCountReadKey \(64 blocks/32768 bytes/1 segment\) so IOBlockStorageDriver splits requests instead of the driver merely rejecting them, sends kIOMessageMediaStateHasChanged\(kIOMediaStateOffline\) via messageClients outside the controller lock after a read error so the family actually decommissions the media, and narrows F5 to the whole-device IOMedia plus one media-path read matching a same-boot baseline; GPT partition discovery is a separate bounded result, Size is in bytes \(61071360\*512 = 31268536320\), and the report omits UUIDs/serials. No code, build, flash or boot; F5 remains \[U\].
- A 2026-11-05 Linux/QEMU qualification run cleared the storage-ABI gate for the actual gta4l candidate ca983f7d. The new board harness scripts/audit-gta4l-storage-interface.py drives the parameterized shared audit \(UniXNU commit 0a1cc71\) and gdb-stop: it stages the existing QEMU ESP with -v serial=3 debug=0x14e keepsym=1 sheng-pmgr=2, stops at \_bsd\_init \(0xfffffff0076e7a3c\), and reads the live IOBlockStorageDevice metaclass 0xfffffff006add1d0 and IOService metaclass 0xfffffff007adb5b8 \(SCTLR\_EL1=0x3454593d, native 4K\). The harness accepts --candidate/--plan/--snapshot/--metaclass-object and the Linux tool paths, uses llvm-dwarfdump, preserves the build's -Wno flags, downgrades clang 24's -Wmissing-designated-field-initializers from -Werror, and maps QEMU's current-EL SCTLR to SCTLR\_EL1. No driver is linked; block I/O, GPT publication and hardware remain unqualified, and F5 remains \[U\]. The negative suite scripts/test-gta4l-storage-abi.py covers the positive audit, six mutated-snapshot refusals, the wrong-candidate refusal and the --skip-qemu run-record checks.
- A 2026-11-05 host batch added the portable read-only block request helper package/Gta4lEmmcBlockCore.c/.h. It uses explicit READ/WRITE/INVALID directions, returns per-request completed bytes \(including a partial final copy\), validates zero, oversized, out-of-range, overflowed and short-buffer requests, rejects writes without touching the callbacks, loops one 512-byte CMD17 read per block through a caller-supplied read\_block callback, copies each block through a caller-supplied copy\_out callback, caps at 64 blocks/32 KiB, marks the media offline on the first read or copy failure and preserves the first failure record and counters. scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py compiles it against scripts/sim-gta4l-emmc-block/harness.c and passes 23 scenarios plus 18 mutation controls. No IOKit, transport or hardware is involved; the C++ adapter, personality, bindings and device run are still ahead, and F5 remains \[U\].
- A 2026-11-05 host batch extracted package/Gta4lEmmcPlatform.c/.h \(MMIO mapping, width-correct register access, barrier and generic-counter timing\) and moved the probe onto it, so package/Gta4lEmmcProbe.c no longer carries window addresses or architectural asm. scripts/build-gta4l-emmc-probe.py now builds libGta4lEmmcCore.a \(core, platform and block helper\) and libGta4lEmmcProbe.a, records both hashes and rejects duplicate global symbols with nm; configs/gta4l\_defconfig force-loads both. Host battery: core 179/104, probe 163/77, block 23/18, and the storage-ABI and board checks pass. The device archives were not built here \(no compile record or xcrun\); no flash or boot, and F5 remains \[U\].
- A 2026-11-05 host batch implemented the P5-1 read-only IOBlockStorageDevice adapter package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp plus its portable policy package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.c/.h, the guarded KernelConfigTables personality patch patches/xnu/gta4l-emmc-block.patch, the archive builder scripts/build-gta4l-emmc-block.py, the host adapter seam scripts/sim-gta4l-emmc-block/adapter\_support.cpp and adapter\_harness.cpp, and the emmc-block-1 diagnostic mapping. The adapter owns one platform/host/helper state and one controller lock, implements the audited 512-byte/read-only override set, advertises 64 blocks/32768 bytes, refuses writes and mode 2, refuses conflicting or duplicate diagnostic tokens before MMIO, and runs a bounded report thread that waits for the whole IOMedia and compares a same-boot baseline sum with a media-path read. The builder compiled the policy object with the bsd\_init.o record and the C++ object with the IOPlatformExpert.cpo record against the qualified storage headers, generated the original storage bindings from candidate ca983f7d \(interface audit 695c7bbe, provenance cbf3a80b, 190 virtual methods, 26 concrete overrides, 0 unresolved original imports\) and archived libGta4lEmmcBlock.a \(fedd4bc3\); it records qualified=true and runtime\_qualified=false. scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py passes 23 core scenarios/18 mutation controls and 28 adapter scenarios against the host IOKit seam; the core/probe suites pass 179/104 and 163/77, and the storage-ABI, payload-contract, inventory and board suites pass. configs/gta4l\_defconfig sets CONFIG\_XNU\_NATIVE\_GTA4L\_EMMC\_BLOCK=n and force-loads the new archive, so the personality is linked but inert until the shared build script translates the key. No kernel or payload image was built, nothing was flashed or booted, no IOMedia object was observed, and F5 remains \[U\]; the device candidate needs a separate BOOT-only approval.
- A 2026-11-05 P5-2 device qualification closed F5 for one reported boot. The D9.4 correction opens the media before the report read \(media-&gt;open\(this, 0, kIOStorageAccessReader\)\), closes it exactly once on every exit and records the access mode; the host IOKit seam starts closed and adds the unopened-read and balanced-open checks, and the block suite is 23 core scenarios/18 mutation controls plus 32 adapter scenarios \(was 28\). F5 is measured PASS at 1-bit SDR, 400 kHz \[C\]; the boot ordinal and cold/warm class were not supplied, and the owner confirmed the tablet returned to TWRP on 2026-11-05. A host-only partition-accounting audit re-used the validated recovery GPT capture in docs/emmc-reference.md \(84 entries x 128 bytes, 80 nonempty partitions, identical primary/backup arrays\) and gpt-report-verified.json \(partition\_count=80, partitions\_equal=true\), and inspected IOGUIDPartitionScheme::scan\(\) and Gta4lEmmcBlock::countPartitions\(\): the scheme skips null-type entries and publishes one non-whole IOMedia per used entry, and the reporter counts non-whole media under the driver. The reported count=80 matches the 80 populated entries and PART1-3 match GPT entries 1-3, so there is no missing-partition or counting defect; expected=84 is the entry-array slot count and remains a misleading label only. Partition completeness is not claimed from the report alone, the accepted p5b artifacts are unchanged, and no reporter/test correction is proposed. - The D10 F2 host batch reworks the optional multi-block mode into the plan's bounded sequence: a two-sector CMD18 request, then one 21-sector CMD18 request over the primary GPT array \(LBAs 2..22\), each explicitly stopped with CMD12 and byte-compared against the same boot's CMD17 reference bytes, followed by bounded CMD17 recovery reads of block 0 and LBA 2. The core records completed sectors separately from the software drain ordinal, and a transfer failure preserves its first record before any stop/reset and stops without a retry or recovery-rung escalation. The report carries per-request requested/completed/validated sectors, first divergence, drain, stop issue/result/status and recovery outcome. The probe simulator now models live FIFO readiness separately from a latched BRR and flags premature drains; new scenarios cover short-request divergence, a mid-transfer readiness stall, an R1 error, failed and diverging recovery reads. Host suites pass core 179/104 and probe 168/77 with the report-width, storage-ABI, payload, package, boot-image, inventory and workflow gates. This is host-only evidence: no candidate image was built, flashed or booted for this batch, and F2 remains \[U\].
- The D10.4a corrective host batch enforces the CMD18 acceptance gate before the candidate build. Each request now has an explicit diagnostic verdict from the preserved transport result, the explicit CMD12 result, the intended length, the completed count and the exact-byte validated count; a content-only failure returns GTA4L\_EMMC\_PROBE\_ERR\_CONTENT \(-21\) without overwriting the transport result or inventing a hardware record, and a length/accounting failure returns GTA4L\_EMMC\_PROBE\_ERR\_LENGTH, now -24 so it cannot collide with GTA4L\_EMMC\_ERR\_TIMING. A failed short request stops before the long CMD18 and the recovery reads, a failed long request stops before recovery, and a failed or diverging block-0 recovery read stops before the LBA-2 read. Recovery state is initialized before any transfer \(result/attempted/compared/same clear, same set only after both requests qualify\) and LBA 0 is disambiguated with recovery\_div\_valid, so a not-run recovery cannot read as success and a later read cannot overwrite the first failure. The hardcoded rc=0 success report is reachable only through one overall gate requiring both request verdicts, both attempts, recovery\_same=1, recovery\_result=OK and both recovery sectors' attempted/compared masks equal to 3. The failure report retains both request summaries, the separate stop results, the eight-field recovery group and the terminal hardware record. New scenarios cover a final-sector mismatch, reordered sectors, a byte mismatch with unchanged aggregate checksum, the second short sector, block-0 and LBA-2 recovery failures, a controlled acceptance seam and a widest failure width form; new mutation controls remove the short and long barriers, the exact-byte comparison, the completed and validated count gates, the recovery transport and divergence early stops and invert the overall gate. Host suites pass core 179/104 and probe 176/84 with thirteen width-bound materializations, plus the report-width, storage-ABI, payload, package, boot-image, inventory and workflow gates. This is host-only evidence: no candidate image was built, flashed or booted for this correction, no device access occurred, and F2 remains \[U\].

- The 2026-11-06 D12 host-only initialization/reliability audit inventoried the H8/H9, A2, H13, H15-H17, p5/p5b and D10.5 records into a 26-row source-bound failure matrix with 15 candidate identities \(docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.42; artifacts under out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-d12-reliability-audit/\). The dominant class is CMD3 completion-silent: a plausible R1 word is present at the timeout while CC is never observed over the full 100,000-iteration \(\~1.14 s\) window, although the same image can complete CMD3 in \~0.3 ms on another boot. Pre-CMD3 status, enables and PRESENT\_STATE match on clean and failing H9 boots, the failing sample has CMD\_INHIBIT clear and ERROR 0, and the A2 attempt-3 post-CMD2 response differs from the timeout response, so the timeout word is not simply the stale CMD2 window. No command-construction, inhibit, CRC/index, CMD1-readiness or W1C defect is demonstrated; the CMD17 TC-only and CMD18 data-timeout classes are separate and p5's client-not-open is a corrected lifecycle defect. Counts are not a controlled rate \(20 direct-record boots, 37 attributed with owner classification, up to 47 by the H13 estimate\), and cold/warm correlation is not established. The disposition is 'no defect demonstrated; missing discriminating observation': a bounded diagnostic-only extension of the existing H9 rung \(pre-CMD3 RESPONSE0..3 plus a log-spaced poll trace, no new probe number and no transport change\) is proposed but not implemented, built, flashed or booted. This is host-only evidence: no device access, commit or push; F2 remains measured PASS for the one D10.5 cold boot and reliability remains unresolved.

- The 2026-11-06 host-only follow-up fixes the H9T kind-column finding recorded above. The sibling UniXNU panic-screen formatter now handles a plain %c by consuming one variadic slot and emitting its low byte through the existing character path; no other conversion, argument order or report-length bound changed. The old formatter hash was 54b950a7. The %c branch alone made the linked image 2615 bytes \(0xa37\) at the D12.5 panic cave, 7 bytes past PANIC\_CAVE\_END; the --panic-screen --no-reset recipe has 2608 bytes \(0xa30\) of padding. The PANIC\_TEXT log path is now compiled only when PANIC\_TEXT is nonzero, which is dead code on the D12.5 kernel \(no \_boot\_progress\_panic\_text\). The guarded formatter is 2519 bytes \(0x9d7\) with 89 bytes spare, and the fixed hash is 8b71ed5a. scripts/test-gta4l-panic-format.py is now a regression test: it pins the fixed hash and the probe format, compares exact output with libc for kinds S/?/C/T/E, an unaffected control and an adjacent %c/%% format, requires a branch-removal mutation to reproduce the old T19  7 and AB7C%DE1234 output, and compiles the full formatter for aarch64-none-elf with the D12.5 cave/symbol addresses, rejecting writable data and requiring at least 32 bytes spare. The pre-fix source fails with the missing-branch message; the unguarded %c source passes behavior but fails the fit phase with 0xa37 bytes and -7 bytes spare; the guarded source passes with 89 bytes spare. No candidate was built, flashed or booted, no device access occurred, the D12.5 image still carries the old formatter, and a new candidate/observation needs fresh approval; F2 and p5b F5 are unchanged and reliability/D13 remain open. Evidence: docs/emmc-panic-format-fix-2026-11-06.md and out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-panic-format-fix/.
- The 2026-11-06 host-only N3 batch implements the D13 chunked report path in the portable block helper and the thin adapter without changing the measured p5b/F5 rung. gta4l-emmc-block=3 selects report-chunked mode, =1 keeps the CMD17 report and =2 remains refused; duplicate block tokens and probe/SDHCI diagnostic conflicts are still refused before MMIO. The helper's optional read\_multi callback returns transport result, requested/completed sectors and stop attempted/result; multi-block requests use two-sector CMD18 chunks with a caller-owned 1024-byte bounce, singleton and odd tails use CMD17, and a chunk is copied out only after zero callback/transport results, exact requested/completed counts, a mandatory stop attempt and a clean stop result. Earlier qualified chunks may contribute partial bytes; a failed chunk contributes none, clears online, latches the first failure, counts chunk/stop failures and suppresses suffix I/O with no retry, CMD17 fallback or in-request recovery. gta4l\_emmc\_read\_multi\_result\(\) snapshots the existing diagnostic multi\_\* fields without changing gta4l\_emmc\_read\_multi\(\) semantics, and the P5 report adds mode=3 plus req/c17/c18/c18b/cfail/stops/sfail/last\_stop inside the 2048-byte bound. the initial N3 suite passed 42 core scenarios with 29 mutation controls and 42 adapter scenarios \(the corrective batch in section 5.3.49 raised this to 48 core/38 mutation/55 adapter plus 3 adapter-source mutations\); the diagnostic payload suite accepts emmc-block-3 through the builder, checker, packager and tests, and the unchanged core/probe suites remain 187/115 and 196/100. The \~128-144 s pathological 64-sector synchronous gate-hold bound is a recorded host-development risk; no timeout, ceiling, watchdog scope, mount, write, DMA/CQE/ICE, width/speed or device result changed. No candidate or archive was built, no QEMU or device access occurred, nothing was flashed, booted, committed or pushed; F2 and p5b F5 are unchanged and N4 requires separate candidate-build and ABI/QEMU approval. Evidence: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.48, docs/emmc-d13-readiness-2026-11-06.md section 2.7 and out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-d13-n3-chunk-evidence/ and out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n3-corrective-evidence/.

- The 2026-11-07 host-only N8.1 design specifies a bounded partition-publication observation for the existing reporter without changing transport, width, timing, writes, mounts, DMA/CQE/ICE or request limits. N5's count=22 expected=84 waited\_ms=0 is an unsynchronized mid-publication snapshot: countPartitions\(\) stops at the first nonzero count and immediately re-samples, and expected is the GPT header entry-array slot count \(84\), not the 80 populated entries. The design chooses an opt-in gta4l-emmc-partobs=1 token orthogonal to gta4l-emmc-block=1\|3; absent, the legacy mode-1 and mode-3 reporter is unchanged. It proposes a 4000 ms deadline, 50 ms sampling interval and 250 ms count-unchanged window, all in the report thread and never under the controller gate, and a PARTOBS line with samples, first/final counts and nominal times, first-nonzero, changes, stability, deadline, timeout, none, error, slots and an explicit complete=0. Host scenarios and mutation controls cover incremental, delayed, stable-partial, late-growth, no-partition, changing, deadline, iterator-error, cancellation, watchdog, legacy, width and no-gate-sleep cases plus the old first-nonzero behavior, false completeness, slot-count substitution, skipped cancellation/error/deadline checks, list-order stability, gate-held sleep and field overflow. The MEDIA zero line is consistent with failure before selection and does not prove the media was absent; gpt=0 is not a corrupt-GPT result and deadline\_ms=2000/timed\_out=0 belong to the later phase. The driver and XFER counters describe incidental scan reads. Source-grounded cause: the pinned IOStorageFamily 67f9003c whole media is created by IOBlockStorageDriver::instantiateMediaObject\(\) through IOMedia::init\(\), which publishes Whole, Size, Content \(after the GUID scheme's Content Mask attaches\), Content Hint, Preferred Block Size and Writable but no Base; kIOMediaBaseKey is set only by partition schemes on partition media, so findExactWholeMedia\(\) rejected the real whole media on every poll and the discovery wait expired. The host seam masked the defect by setting Base on every media; the partcmp-property BASE\_MISSING control faults a partition media, not the whole media. The owner's subjective extra log delay is consistent with the nominal 20,000 ms discovery wait but is not a measured duration. No driver fix, rebuild, flash, retry, reset/repair, commit or push; no partition-completeness or reliability claim, and N8/N8.5 complete=0 is not upgraded. The host seam now pins the hardware signature with the partcmp-whole-no-base scenario \(adapter suite 86 scenarios/16 adapter and eight policy mutation controls; core 48/38\). The proposed owner-gated correction is to accept an absent Base on a whole media as the implied zero base while still rejecting a present nonzero Base, keeping the Size/Content/ancestry and exactly-one guards. Findings: docs/emmc-n9.4-partcmp-observation-2026-11-07.md and docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.59.
- The 2026-11-07 host-only N9.4 correction applied the owner-approved whole-media Base fix in package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp \(b7aeff0a\): findExactWholeMedia\(\) now fetches the raw Base property and accepts an absent Base on a whole media as the implied zero, while a present property that is not an OSNumber or is nonzero is still rejected; the Whole/Size/Content/ancestry and exactly-one guards are unchanged, snapshotPartitionMedia\(\) still requires Base on partition media, and the legacy findWholeMedia\(\) still tests only Whole and ancestry. The host seam inverts partcmp-whole-no-base to require the full successful comparison \(whole=1 base=0, gpt=1 reason=0, media=80 match=1, complete=1\) and adds partcmp-whole-base-invalid, which rejects a present nonzero Base and a present wrong-type Base with reason=10 before any read; partcmp-property still faults a partition media. The block suite passes 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 87 adapter scenarios with 16 adapter and eight policy mutation controls. This is host-only evidence: no candidate rebuild, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, retry, reset/repair, commit or push was performed, and N8/N8.5 complete=0 is not upgraded. N9.5 production remains separately gated. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md sections 5.3.59-5.3.60 and docs/emmc-n9.4-partcmp-observation-2026-11-07.md section 5.

- S1 speed is design-only and blocked \(2026-11-12\): the 1 MiB/32 KiB draft was withdrawn because GTA4L\_EMMC\_BLOCK\_CHUNK\_BLOCKS is two, so one 32 KiB request is 32 sequential chunks, and the separate width/timing revert bounds do not fit the 30,000 ms watchdog. The corrected 4 KiB design needs a combined width/timing revert helper. Host helper batch \(2026-11-12\): gta4l\_emmc\_recover\_width\_and\_timing\(\) now drops the clock to 400 kHz, clears a stuck data inhibit, writes HS\_TIMING=0 and BUS\_WIDTH=1, clears the host width bits, and verifies byte 185 and the read-only properties in one EXT\_CSD read. Eighteen core scenarios cover clean 4-bit and 8-bit, clock refusal, stuck HS, HS CMD6 error, width CMD6 error, properties mismatch, surviving inhibit, inhibit-clear success, final read timeout and argument checks, plus enforced-deadline expiry in the clock wait, data reset, command-inhibit wait, CMD6 poll, TRAN poll and final read; the core suite passes 207 scenarios and 126 mutation controls. The S1 block-driver mode is still not implemented and no S1 image may be flashed.
- B10.5 SMMU decode \(2026-11-14\): the one owner boot of a1e26c34 completed the read-only gate without an abort and reported stream 0x00c0 matched by SMR\(0\)=0x800000c0 with S2CR\(0\)=0x00000000 \(type translate, CBNDX 0\). DMA was skipped \(cmp=0/0, chunks=0, skip=1\) and no ADMA2 transfer has been issued from XNU. ID0=0x4c017e32 reports S1TS=1, S2TS=0, NTS=0 with 50 stream groups, consistent with a hypervisor-virtualized SMMU that owns stage 2; the mainline Qualcomm bypass quirk probably applies. Context bank 0 CBAR/SCTLR were not read, so identity versus firmware page tables is unresolved; B10.7 reads them read-only and runs DMA only if the bank is provably translation-off. PIO read 9797632 bytes in 4.009 s and took the controlled budget exit; the prefix digest 62ddc953 matches the image. Post-boot p69, RECOVERY, EFS and sec\_efs hashes are unchanged.
- B10.7 SMMU census \(2026-11-14\): stream 0x00c0 translates through context bank 0. The B10.7 candidate reads ID1/ID2/sGFSR/sGFSYNR0, every valid SMR up to 128 groups, CBAR/CBA2R and the matched bank's SCTLR/TCR2/TTBR0/TCR/MAIR0/FSR/FAR/FSYNR0, plus the last bank, all read-only. Only cb-off \(TYPE S1\_TRANS\_S2\_BYPASS, SCTLR.M=0, no CB FSR fault bit, one match, valid layout\) allows DMA; layout/conflict/cb-type/translate-live/cb-fault skip. The owner boot of 0d77d602 reached the gate: ID1=50000030 \(48 context banks, 64 global pages\), ID2=00005111, five valid SMRs with one match and two translate streams sharing CB0, CBAR=0001f000 TYPE 1, SCTLR=000000e0 M=0, TCR/TTBR0=0, FSR=00000400 \(FORMAT only, no fault bit\) and sGFSR=0. The gate returned cb-fault only because it treated the nonzero FORMAT field as a fault, so DMA was skipped \(cmp=0/0, skip=1\) and PIO exited on its fixed budget at BULKFAIL stage=6; the GR1/CB reads did not abort and shap=lk hashed 11,665,408 bytes in about 98 ms. The correct verdict was cb-off; B10.11 masks CB FSR with the mainline FAULT set \(0xc00001fe\), keeps FORMAT \(0x600\) report-only and fixes the same predicate in the post-DMA gate.
- B10.11 CB FSR FORMAT gate \(2026-11-14\): the B10.7 owner boot proved CB0 is S1\_TRANS\_S2\_BYPASS with SCTLR.M=0 and that the hardware leaves FSR=0x400, which is FORMAT \(bits 10:9\) and not a fault. The pre-DMA gate and the post-DMA re-read now mask FSR with the mainline FAULT set 0xc00001fe, keep FORMAT 0x600 report-only, and still fail closed on TF/SS/MULTI and any sGFSR bit. Host fixtures start from 0x400; new FORMAT/TF/SS rows and a post-DMA FORMAT row pass, and the four mask mutations are rejected. The bulk SPEED line omits the six zero S1-only fields, shrinking the report worst case from 3,208 to 3,040 bytes inside the unchanged 3,328-byte buffer. Candidate kernel 9f72210f after the audit-&gt;relink fixpoint from cace0f26 \(same 190 slots, 181 bindings, vtable fffffff005cb44c0, metaclass fffffff006add1d0 and service fffffff007b3f5b8\), storage-ABI out/gta4l/storage-abi-9f72210f PASS, requalified archive 6075549b with members e8b3ecc7/3d1a1e1f matching the kernel-build report, payload 91ff0a18 \(83,886,080 bytes; emmc-block-3-bulk-dma; gate 105/105\) and BOOT image fcb1603b \(85,688,320 bytes; gate 18/18, only the ramdisk differs\). Not flashed; the first DMA boot needs an owner go naming fcb1603b.
- B10.12 cache maintenance and failure-only DMADIAG \(2026-11-14\): the ADMA2 path now stores the descriptor-table page and the bounce buffer plus canary with IOMemoryDescriptor::performOperation\(kIOMemoryIncoherentIOStore\) before the command, flushes the buffer range with kIOMemoryIncoherentIOFlush before the CPU comparison, and records TABLE\_STORE/BUFFER\_STORE/BUFFER\_FLUSH in the DMA report. The pinned XNU is built with the BCM2837 board config, which leaves \_\_ARM\_COHERENT\_IO\_\_ undefined, so the call dispatches to the real dcache\_incoherent\_io\_store64/flush64 clean and clean+invalidate routines. Every DMA failure now fills the failure-only DMADIAG line with table/buffer physicals, entry and descriptor-byte counts, SDHCI error/normal/present, command-time HC1/HC2 and ADMA address, and the first 32 table bytes, plus the dc maintenance bits. The host fake controller is non-coherent by default \(shadow DRAM, Store/Flush gating\) and rejects dropped or mis-sized maintenance. - Storage milestone mapping to the UniXNU guide \(2026-11-15\): hardware inventory = the S0 SoC/board inventory rows; host access = S1 SPEED \(register, clock and link state at HS 50 MHz with a verified revert\); DMA and commands = the B10 ADMA2/SMMU path \(the S6 rung-a pulled into S2\); block reads = S2 BULK 80/80, S3 LIVE and the S4 WINDOWS read-only rung; root = S5 eMMC root; persistence = S7 write path and the post-S5 log region; performance = S8 against the mainline Linux sdhci-msm driver. The S4 SHNGROOT parser is aligned with ShengUfs RootLayout.h by refusing version 2 explicitly with a sealed-header test and requiring a 4096-multiple image\_bytes; GPT-name discovery is not adopted because the PARTMAP rung already compares the published range set with the primary GPT and a range is unique in a valid GPT \(docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.132\).
- S5 VerifiedRoot decision \(2026-11-15\): a raw iPadOS 17.7.11 root mount is refused before userspace by the rootvp authentication panic; the unchanged original launchd starts only with the owner root binding accepted, the original AMFI/crypto tables genuine and zero media integrity failures. The eMMC OS window must therefore be published through a verified path with an owner-signed policy; every 4096-byte block is checked, the signed volume UUID must equal 04769D26-2CA0-4825-B443-E2E5F88300F2, and the kernel needs XNU\_OWNER\_ROOT\_AUDIT/XNU\_OWNER\_ROOT\_TRUST plus the native\_verified\_block\_\* symbols exported by the window path. The OS window hash table is 1,483,785 x 32 = 47,481,120 bytes \(45.28 MiB; the last policy block is 1,536 bytes\) and cannot fit beside the 83,886,080-byte diagnostic payload in the 100,663,296-byte BOOT partition, so S5 must store it as a version-2 OS.vroot data object in userdata \(block size 4096, flags 3\) and repeat the R2 write. S4 keeps the version-1 image fd43ee70 and publishes its windows without owner verification, so it makes no root-authentication claim \(docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.133\).
- S4 window publication \(2026-11-15\): the rung now prepares, starts and waits for one read-only block service per SHNGROOT window, using extra Gta4lEmmcBlock instances in a window role because the build script audits only that vtable. The plan starts SystemOS first and OS last; a window is ready only when a 36-character UUID media provider chain reaches the window and passes through an Apple\_APFS container media \(type GUID 7c3457ef-0000-11aa-aa11-00306543ecac\), which excludes the GPT partition UUID and selects the APFS volume. The wait is 100 ms x 20 per window, statically under the watchdog with the 1000 ms read budget and 2000 ms margin; failures discard only never-started windows. WINDOWS carries pub/phase/perr and the OS volume UUID; a valid manifest keeps valid=1 reason=0 when publication fails. The formatter line is 598 bytes and the S4 worst case 1986 bytes. Host suite 511 scenarios \(512 PASS lines\) with 192 adapter, 3 support and 52 policy mutation controls. Not yet: the windows evidence checker, the S4 candidate, APFS prelink verification and the S5 verified path \(docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.134\).

The device write and verify boot each need the owner go. No device access, flash or userdata write.
- S5c′ fixes the consumed S5c verdict. The device transcript showed the v2 windows path passing \(pub=2 phase=5 wdiag=15\) while VERIFY failed with reason=3 after exactly 1,449 full 32 KiB chunks of OS.vroot: the 288-byte tail was issued below the 512-byte media block. The loader now rounds every table request to 512 bytes via gta4l\_emmc\_block\_verify\_request\_bytes, the verify rung qualifies the B10 ADMA2 path \(SMMU gate, four-case PIO comparison, post-DMA fault check; setup failure is terminal with windows reason 20\), a failed verify publishes nothing \(pub=0 perr=5\) and clears the consumer state, and the failure VERIFY line reports vobj/voff/vlen/vio/vact with dma= on WINDOWS/VERIFY. Candidate kernel fdc52902..., archive 70931301..., payload 06e0fcce... \(emmc-block-3-verify plus gta4l-emmc-dma=1\) and BOOT d373c0d5...; userdata stays ae6e18e7... and the p69 read-only preflight matched the manifest and both vroot objects. The owner go selected BOOT d373c0d5..., which was flashed BOOT-only to p69; the read-back image region matched, the after-image was zero and RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs were unchanged. The single verdict boot passed: VERIFY valid=1 reason=0 os=1 sysos=1 blocks=1025 ms=10968 fail=4294967295 dma=1 with osha=0fb9adee... and ssha=d3257aae... matching the host envelopes; WINDOWS pub=2 phase=5 perr=0 wdiag=15 dma=1 rms=472 rch=129; SMMU cb-off with FORMAT-only FSR; controller clean. The boot is consumed. ms leaves 1,032 ms of the 12,000 ms budget, so S5c″ adds per-phase timers and removes redundant table hashing. S5c″ also records that vr\_verify\_block currently runs only in the sampled edges \(published-window reads are not verified per request\) and that native\_ufs\_block\_authenticates is not exported; both block S5d/S5e. Post-boot TWRP re-measure matched p69 d373c0d5..., the manifest, both vroot objects and the protected partitions.
- S5d is host-complete. Shared UniXNU commits d20cfc5 \(XNU\_VERIFIED\_BLOCK\_PROVIDER selects virtio or gta4l-emmc; owner root trust requires a provider, and the gta4l provider skips the VirtIO probe archive\) and 278cd83 \(owner flags change patched bsd\_init/kern\_exec/vm\_unix/IOKitBSDInit -D values, so the script drops those objects, the linked kernel and the personality table when the flag identity moves\) are on branch gta4l-s5d-verified-root-provider. The defconfig enables XNU\_VERIFIED\_ROOT\_PROBE, XNU\_OWNER\_ROOT\_AUDIT, XNU\_OWNER\_ROOT\_TRUST and XNU\_VERIFIED\_BLOCK\_PROVIDER="gta4l-emmc"; run unixnu defconfig after editing it because the build reads the output-tree .config. bsd\_init.o records all three flags as 1. The driver adds gta4l-emmc-root=1 \(requires block=3, windows, verify and DMA; conflicts with other diagnostics\); a successful verified publication sets reportDone and logs GTA4L-EMMC P5 ROOT: online=1 instead of the terminal panic, while a pre-root failure still panics. native\_verified\_root\_probe is a logging stub because the block archive supplies the verified-block exports. Kernel d5d463c3..., archive 228577ac..., storage-ABI audit out/gta4l/storage-abi-d5d463c3 PASS \(190 slots/181 bindings, vtable/metaclass unchanged, service fffffff007b4b5b8\). Block suite exit=0 \(297 PASS lines, adapter 526, core 47/35\). Cache/Data mount flags and the RAM Data UUID wait for S5e. No candidate, flash, boot or device access.

### 完成条件

- Host: register, clock, link and SMMU facts match the board inventory.
- Reads: SCSI capacity, GPT and known blocks are correct; OS/SystemOS windows stay within userdata.
- Root: the configured volume mounts and launchd starts. Persistence: writes survive reboot within the allocated Data region.

### 当前范围

- A read-only XNU block-driver implementation exists and is host-tested, but no XNU block-device result exists yet. Recovery inventory is read-only evidence and does not establish register, clock, DMA, power-domain or SMMU behavior under XNU. Recovery regulator state is not the XNU handoff state; the L24 reading was internally inconsistent \(state=disabled with num\_users=1 and microvolts=2960000\), so it does not prove the eMMC VDD rail is on at the audit point in bsd\_init.
- The v5 snapshot and handoff audit are register-level diagnostics. They do not establish the PMIC L24/L11 state, VDDCX power domain, interconnect bandwidth, SMMU stream ID or card protocol state.
- The pinned mainline port is source evidence only. Its no-supply sdhc\_1 design does not measure L24/L11 at XNU handoff and is not an XNU regulator control or readback path; the separate SPMI map in docs/pm6125-register-map.md is a register map, not an implemented readback.
- The NativeEmmc audit is static source review only. It does not establish that the reviewed driver builds, probes or reads on any device, and its MediaTek MSDC target does not exercise the gta4l SDHCI v5/CQE/ICE path, clock/reset/power sequencing, interconnect or SMMU. Its firmware-left-controller assumption is not satisfied by the gta4l handoff, and its hardcoded LBA and UUIDs are image-specific.
- S1 qualifies the first 4 KiB of BOOT on one boot at 8-bit / 50 MHz with HISPD clear and a verified pre-report revert only. It does not qualify sustained or arbitrary I/O, throughput beyond those two passes, the full BOOT region, writes, persistence, DMA, interrupts, CQE, HS200/HS400, a filesystem, a mount, repeatability, initialization reliability, the electrical timing margin, pad or DLL state, or lifetime fast mode; the cold/warm class and boot ordinal were not recorded, and the 1-bit comparison is a wire-time bound, not a measured sample.
- S2 bulk run 1 \(image c6e23141\) is INCOMPLETE: a controlled 4000 ms stream-budget exit after 279 of 2615 64-block requests, with a verified 9142272-byte prefix digest and no I/O error. The data phase is 184.6 us per 512-byte block, 18.0x the 8-bit / 50 MHz wire time; inhibit, command, stop and the IOKit path together are under 1 percent of the stream. The full region projects to about 37 s including hashing and does not fit the 30000 ms watchdog. The full region, the 80 windows, sustained I/O, a throughput claim, repeatability and the effective clock/width versus MMIO-drain cause remain unqualified.
- S2.1 run 1 \(image fec0f921\) is INCOMPLETE: a controlled 4000 ms stream-budget exit after 280 of 2615 64-block requests \(9175040 verified bytes\). The BULKIO split is brr=6003260 ticks \(0.313 s, including 0.226 s of 10 us delays\), drain=57138691 ticks \(2.976 s, 90.4 percent of the data phase\), sha=13203697 ticks \(0.688 s\); the drain is 166.1 us per 512-byte block, or 1.30 us per 32-bit FIFO read. hc=20 and cc=0007 confirm the programmed 8-bit host width and enabled clock; rcg=0x20B is the selected 50 MHz floor row, whose parent rate remains a table value. The small BRR wait relative to the drain points at the host MMIO drain, not the card. S2 remains unqualified: full region, 80 windows, sustained rate, repeatability and the DMA decision are open; the next batch targets ready-block draining, direct 32-bit stores, inlined data-port reads and a DRAINCAL timing.
- S2.2 run 1 \(image cbc0ee1e\) is INCOMPLETE: a controlled 4000 ms stream-budget exit after 292 of 2615 64-block requests \(9568256 verified bytes\) with no I/O error and a verified revert. The stream loop is 4.000 s \(2.39 MB/s end to end\); the CMD18 data phase is 3.249 s and the FIFO drain 3.115 s \(95.9 percent\) at 166.7 us per 512-byte block, or 1.30 us per 32-bit read. The idle data-port calibration cal=3154 ticks over 128 reads is 1.28 us per read, about the in-stream drain cost, so the PIO drain is host-MMIO-bound, not card-paced. The full region projects to about 35.8 s and cannot fit the 30000 ms watchdog; PIO cannot meet S2 and polled ADMA2 is the next rung. The S2.1 3.08 MB/s figure is the drain-only rate; its end-to-end rate is 2.29 MB/s. DRV online=0 is expected for a non-clean bulk stage. The full region, the 80 windows, sustained rate, repeatability, DMA, a mount and lifetime fast mode remain unqualified.
- The B10.5 gate establishes only that stream 0x00c0 is routed to context bank 0 for translation at XNU handoff; it does not establish the bank type, SCTLR.M, TTBR/TCR, the hypervisor stage-2 mapping, or that DMA can safely use any address. DMA remains unqualified: no ADMA2 transfer has run from XNU. A hypervisor-virtualized SMMU with S2TS=0 is inferred from ID0, and the Qualcomm bypass quirk is probable but unproven on this device. The PIO fallback is still MMIO-bound \(2.44 MB/s, 4.009 s for 9797632 bytes\) and cannot cover the region under the 30000 ms watchdog. The full region, the 80 windows, sustained rate, repeatability, writes, interrupts, CQE, HS200/HS400, a mount and lifetime fast mode remain unqualified.
- B10.11 booted once and failed the first ADMA read in ST\_FDS \(cmp=0/4, ast=1, aaddr=tp+0x18, desc=0xffffffff\) without capturing the SDHCI error/normal/present, physicals, HC1/HC2 or ADMA readback, so the cause is not established. B10.12 added performOperation Store/Flush maintenance for the table and the buffer plus canary, a non-coherent host model and a failure-only DMADIAG capture. The single B10.12 boot \(BOOT ddbe2eb7\) returned rc=0 stop=0 wp=1 with DMA cmp=4/4 canary=1 poison=1, chunks=2615 bytes=85688320 fails=0, the full 80/80-window BULK digest, a verified 1-bit/400 kHz revert and no DMADIAG line, confirming the missing cache maintenance as the B10.11 cause; it is one boot, not repeatability or lifetime evidence. That boot still read the bulk stream and windows through dmaRead\(\) directly and served normal multi-block reads with PIO, so S2 is transport-proven with the block-stack DMA path open. B10.13 routes normal multi-block reads and the bulk stream/windows through the block-stack read path under the one controller gate, latches DMA off after the first failure, and adds req/blk counters; it is built, host-gated and flashed once to p69 \(BOOT a4809df8, post-write p69 73278c8f\) with one owner-controlled cold boot pending. cb-off is still the only gate decision that allows DMA; SMMU writes \(B10.8\) remain a separate R2 approval. PIO is still MMIO-bound \(2.44 MB/s\), and repeatability, writes, interrupts, CQE, HS200/HS400, a mount and lifetime fast mode remain unqualified.
- B10.13 closes S2 on one owner boot: the block-stack DMA path carried the full 85688320-byte region and 80/80 windows with req=2695 blk=167520, no DMADIAG line and a verified 1-bit/400 kHz revert. It is still one boot, so repeatability, sustained lifetime service, writes, interrupts, CQE, HS200/HS400, a mount and fast mode remain unqualified. cb-off remains the only gate decision that allows DMA; SMMU writes \(B10.8\) remain a separate R2 approval. The 4000 ms stream budget now has 20.4 percent headroom \(3.322 s measured\) instead of 30.8 percent; any budget change needs a documented bound and margin, and the 30000 ms watchdog stays unchanged.
- S3a is measured PASS on two boots but is not repeatability or lifetime evidence. The force-slow path has no measured 400 kHz/1-bit timing sample: the 3.599 s bound is clock-derived from 322 blocks at 50 kB/s plus command, host and drain allowances, and the 4,500 ms cap leaves 901 ms; a first hardware slow run, if ever authorized, must report ticks before the bound is treated as measured. S3a's LIVE line is visible only because the rung ends in the panic report, and S3b lifetime service cannot be observed on device without a visible log channel, so fast mode stays non-default until that channel or S5. The 3,584-byte report buffer is now a linked-candidate measurement, but the panic screen was full on both S3a boots \(FAIL was the last visible row; pages/free were off screen\), so the report trim is required before S4 adds WINDOWS:.

### 下一步

- The host-local milestone is complete and the mainline RPM/regulator source trace is recorded in docs/rpm-power-interface.md. The pinned port has no L24/L11 consumer and no RPM readback; it relies on retained firmware rails. A separate SPMI readback map is recorded in docs/pm6125-register-map.md. Keep bus power and card clock off and issue no card commands, DMA or storage writes. Do not implement an RPM vote, SPMI regulator write or bus-power acknowledgement to learn the state. Next decision: accept a retained-rail contract only with the written board-level electrical contract and a direct rail measurement; a PMIC readback reports commanded state, not measured voltage, and substitutes for neither; the Mu-Silicium exit trace in docs/firmware-handoff.md shows the handoff writes no LDO control register but does not measure the rails, so it does not unblock the power stage. Keep the working RAM root throughout. Step 4 records the electrical-contract gap assessment in docs/sdhc-electrical-contract.md and Step 6 records NO-GO in docs/sdhc-decision-memo.md; do not treat the passing host-local tests or the oracle as permission for bus-power or card-clock activation. The next legitimate work is host-only evidence acquisition \(datasheets, schematic, firmware/RPM analysis\) and, only under a separately approved measurement procedure, direct rail/pad measurement. A bounded RPM/XBL static trace is recorded in docs/power-retention-evidence.md §7: it identifies the RPM LDOA resource tables for L11/L24, finds no referenced static table or stored pointer/resolvable PC-relative literal that selects either rail in the bounded scan, and finds no direct L11/L24 voltage, mode or enable write in the traced paths; the setter's class object and per-id array are built at runtime, and the runtime policy, PBS semantics and retention remain unresolved, so E7 stays blocking. The NativeEmmc audit in docs/native-emmc-reference-audit.md is a design reference for the read-only XNU block-device shape. The host-side SDHCI v5/eMMC core is now implemented and host-tested in docs/emmc-driver.md; it performs controller reset, GCC 400 kHz setup, status-enable polling, separate bus/IO power requests that require an observed BUS\_ON completion, an explicit fixed-1.8 V signalling and pad-supply policy, CMD3 RCA assignment, normalized SDHCI long responses, 512-byte sector-size enforcement, enumeration, capacity/partition validation and single-block PIO reads only when gta4l-emmc-probe=1 is set. Stages 2 and 3 stop after host init and identification respectively. The stage-2 and stage-3 probes have run on the tablet; the stage-3 failure was audited against the pinned references and the four host-code fixes are applied and host-tested. The host-only CMD2 CRC audit in docs/emmc-cmd2-crc-audit.md ranks the remaining hypotheses and finds no reference-supported software correction; CRC checking stays enabled. The 2026-11-01 cmd1gap candidate completed full stage-3 identification on three units, including the owner's SM-T505N where the fail-closed image had failed CMD2; the 8-us delay is the only source delta and is not proven necessary. The 2026-11-02 stage-1 image completed identification and the read-only block 0/1 CMD17 reads on the owner's SM-T505N and one SM-T505, and the stage-A rebuild then read the full 84-entry primary GPT array \(LBAs 2..22\), the first usable sector and the last sector on the owner's SM-T505N; every per-LBA sum matched a same-session read-only recovery readback, so the Stage-A/F1 content claim is closed. The 2026-11-02 optional A2/F2 attempt \(gta4l-emmc-mb=1\) stopped before the multi-block phase at the known CMD3-completion timeout, so no F2 result exists; the post-attempt audit found and fixed a missing SDHCI Multi/Single Block Select bit in the CMD18 transfer-mode word, and a corrected image is required for a second attempt. Bus width \(F3\), high-speed timing \(F4\), filesystem/partition verification, block-device publication \(F5\), DMA, CQE, ICE, write path \(F6\) and microSD remain absent. The next rung is a B1 diagnostic for the 4-bit readback failure, or a separately planned A2/F2/bus-width variant; any device step needs separate owner approval and a verified EFS/CPEFS backup, and no block-device publication, filesystem mount, DMA, CQE, ICE, write path or microSD path exists. As of 2026-11-02, the attempt-3 drain was exercised on a later boot without resolving F2, and the Stage-B1 bus-width/bus-test rung is host-tested but the 2026-11-02 three-boot hardware run is a failing result: only one boot reached B1 and it stopped at the 4-bit byte-183 readback \(sw=0, swrc=-19\), so F3 remains unproven. The 2026-11-02 cold session then reproduced the 4-bit stop in 4/4 B1-reaching boots \(2 warm, 2 cold\), excluded a retained warm-reset card state, and bound all eight boots through a matching p69 re-read; the next rung is the owner-approved B1C continuation \(gta4l-emmc-probe=5\), planned in section 3.1.3 of the ladder plan but not yet built, and F3 remains unproven pending its device run. The B1C image \(gta4l-emmc-probe=5\) was built from clean 5ad1dd6, flashed BOOT only to p69 \(pre ac356ef1, post c6a9f8e0\) and booted seven times without reflashing on 2026-11-03; the same-session p69 re-read after boot 3 matched the post-flash hash. Two warm boots reached the continuation on exactly the pinned 4-bit tuple and both timed out in the CMD19 write phase \(tt=1, tw=0, te=0, trc=-11; CC and BufferWriteReady latched, no TC, no error; present 0x03f801f6\) - the project's first data-write-direction terminal sample - while the other five stopped pre-B1 \(four byte-identical CMD17 TC-only/no-error timeouts and one H8 CMD3 timeout\). The timeout repeated across a full cold power cycle and is deterministic conditional on reaching the rung; the hard error skipped the content pass and byte-183 re-read \(e183a/esa are placeholders\), and es=000009cb still reads at the attempted 4-bit width. F3 remains unproven: neither a width-caused timeout nor an independent first-write property is excluded. The other eleven boots stopped pre-B1 \(eight H8 CMD3 timeouts, three CMD17 TC-only/no-error timeouts, one at the GPT array LBA 2 - a new position\); trc=-20 remains unobserved, the byte-183 paradox is unchanged and F3 stays \[U\]. The owner-chosen next rung is a host data-timeout audit \(probe=7\): both hang classes record no data-timeout interrupt despite ERROR\_INT\_ENABLE carrying the DTO bit, so the rung reads back the timeout/enable state, arms the minimum timeout for a healthy 8-block CMD18+CMD12 read longer than the counter, re-reads block 0 and repeats the 1-bit CMD19 wedge under an 8 s software bound. The boot ordinal and cold/warm class were not supplied; the owner confirmed TWRP recovery on 2026-11-05, and a host-only partition-accounting audit found count=80 matches the 80 populated GPT entries \(expected=84 is the slot count\), so no correction is proposed and partition completeness is not claimed from the report alone. F2, 8-bit, 4-bit/HS publication, mount/root switch and F6 remain separate gates, and no observed mount, root switch, persistent Data path, DMA, CQE, ICE or microSD path exists. The D10.5 F2 candidate then passed on the owner-reported first cold boot \(both CMD18 requests byte-validated, clean CMD12 stops and matching CMD17 recovery reads; docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.41\), so F2 is measured PASS for that one boot at 1-bit SDR, 400 kHz \[C\] while repeatability, cold-start reliability and multi-block publication remain open. Hardware exit is two separately authorized boots \(cold, then warm-from-held\) plus a TWRP integrity check; fast mode does not become the default until both pass.
- S3 live design is revised to R1-R3 in docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.125 revision 2 \(host-only; no code, candidate, flash or boot\). The force-slow token skips the stream and runs the existing 80-window set \(80 two-block media reads plus 160 single-block CMD17 references = 320 blocks\) plus one 2-block re-read at 1-bit/400 kHz PIO; the clock-derived bound is 3.599 s with the 4,500 ms bulk total as the cap \(901 ms margin\), and no 400 kHz/1-bit timing sample exists. Recovery uses one liveState field under the gate: the failing request sets RECOVERING before releasing the gate, new requests wait gate-free up to the 9,500 ms recovery budget plus margin, and the recovery thread calls gta4l\_emmc\_recover\_width\_and\_timing\(\) directly \(not speedRecover, which takes the gate itself\). S3 is split: S3a is a held-terminal panic report with LIVE held=1 revert=0 and one owner go naming the image SHA-256 and approving two boots of that image \(cold, then power + volume-down warm reset\) plus a read-only TWRP check; S3b is the lifetime default and waits for a visible log channel or S5. The LIVE line needs the report buffer raised from 3,328 to 3,584 bytes \(bulk success worst 3,070 plus the LIVE worst\) with new static asserts. Next: implement the selectors, S3a held path, liveState/recovery code, LIVE line, host scenarios/mutations and LIVE checker shapes; then the candidate chain. Do not boot a4809df8, fcb1603b, ddbe2eb7, 0d77d602 or a1e26c34 again.
- S3a is measured PASS on two boots and the two-boot budget is consumed; do not boot 97ca05c6 again. The close-out is recorded in docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.127. Next is the standing report trim: derive the panic-screen row capacity and fail any mode whose worst-case wrapped report plus panic frames and pages/free exceeds it, compacting SMMU/SMMUCB/BULKIO/DMA/SPEED to the checker-required fields while keeping every field the checkers read. S4 windows follows: host userdata dry-run/image build first, then the read-only OS/SystemOS publication and WINDOWS line; the whole-userdata write needs its own go naming the image SHA-256 plus the backup decision. S3b stays deferred per R3.
- S5 VerifiedRoot is required: build the owner policy \(header and signature in the payload, 47,481,120-byte OS.vroot table in a version-2 userdata manifest\), export the native\_verified\_block\_\* symbols and the weak native\_ufs\_block\_authenticates hook from the window path, enable XNU\_OWNER\_ROOT\_AUDIT/XNU\_OWNER\_ROOT\_TRUST, then re-cut and rewrite userdata under a fresh owner go. Keep the private owner key outside the tree; embed only the public anchor. S4 stays on the version-1 image and report-only window publication.

- `source`: [Records the native SDHCI v5/eMMC core after the third and stage-3 audit fixes: bus/IO power requests are handled separately, success requires an observed BUS\_ON completion and the latest acknowledged IO level to match the desired HOST\_CONTROL2 level \(a later opposite level, a standalone BUS\_ON, contradictory bits or a wrong level raised despite an unchanged VDD\_180 bit invalidate an earlier completion; the ack does not clear request latches\), a bus-only request is acknowledged with both BUS\_SUCCESS and IO\_SUCCESS as in Linux, and power-loop exhaustion returns ERR\_POWER; the fixed-1.8 V signalling and pad-supply policy is explicit and documented as a departure from the DT-derived Linux state; the probe aborts on a stalled counter; SDHCI long responses are normalized and the response-type mask keeps SHORT\_BUSY from being parsed as R2; 4 KiB data sectors and reserved DATA\_SECTOR\_SIZE encodings are rejected; non-busy command responses and data FIFO drains are followed by a final pre-clear error re-read as well as the CMD7 terminal capture; poll loops use the documented 10 us step, where the microsecond value is a nominal iteration budget rather than a counter deadline, and on the busy-completion success path the CMD7 terminal capture is followed by a final error-status re-read that bounds errors asserted during its diagnostic reads; capacity/partition validation and the staged opt-in XNU probe remain at that point; the single-block CMD17 PIO reads were exercised later on hardware \(below\). The stage-2 probe ran on the tablet on 2026-10-31 and reported GTA4L-EMMC HOST REPORT rc=0 mci=10000072 pwr=0f clk=0007 hc2=0008 ven=00318a0c pst=00000000 log=4/1/268435570 \(photo sha256 d049547e8fd6135aafe6fb8aff13c9e8295cb9e2012b3944cdb66ac364882a2c\); BOOT only, readback matched and recovery stayed byte-identical. This establishes controller reset, bus/IO power completion, pad policy and card clock; card identification, sector reads, rail voltages and retention remain unproven, and no block device, filesystem, DMA, CQE, ICE or write path exists. The later stage-3 identification attempt failed with ERR\_CMD\_CRC; the follow-up audit added the GCC SDCC1 block reset, the vendor POR 0xA9C restore, the PWRSAVE\_DLL clear, the low-speed DLL\_RST/DLL\_PDN pair and terminal-failure capture, a second audit added clock-gated failure diagnostics and retirement of recovered CMD1 timeouts, and the probe now reports the DLL registers and terminal failure record. The later diagnostics-only cmd2window run passes CMD2 and stops at a CMD3 completion timeout \(rc=-7\); the expanded fmt=2 capture ran on hardware on 2026-10-31 and passed CMD3 and CMD9, then stopped at a CMD7 R1b completion timeout with TC set and CC absent \(see docs/emmc-cmd7-timeout-finding.md\). The 2026-11-02 stage-1 image completed identification and both read-only CMD17 reads on the owner's SM-T505N and one SM-T505; the reports carry step=15, log=7, the recovery-known CSD, and block 0/block 1 words that decode to a protective MBR and a GPT header. This is single-block content evidence, not a filesystem, reliability, publication or write result.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-driver.md)

The test copies package/Gta4lEmmcProbe.c, replaces only the generic-counter/barrier instructions and the six MMIO helpers with recording hooks, and links it with minimal kernel-header stubs and simulated SDHCI/GCC windows. The CMD7 scenarios cover the transcribed TC-only timeout, changed and unchanged response windows, CC-then-TC, TC-then-CC, simultaneous completion, CC-only bounded timeout, busy-phase error, late busy error, an error asserted during the terminal diagnostic reads on the busy-completion success path and bounded-trace overflow. Three report-max-width end-to-end runs execute the failure, identification and full-probe success report forms with wide simulated values \(a 10-digit capacity, both poll budgets at their limit, 16-hex-digit tick fields, a full four-entry CMD7 trace and its overflow count\) and require the report to stay below the 2048-byte panic-text budget; they are execution samples, not a field-wise width bound. Three field-wise report-width scenarios call the production formatters from scripts/sim-gta4l-emmc-probe/report-width-fixture.c with bounded extremes \(ten-digit counter, six-digit poll budgets, late CC/TC, late stored trace index, maximum overflow, 16-hex-digit ticks and twenty CMD1 attempts\), assert every bounded field, and require the measured length plus margin to fit the buffer; a width-budget control checks the 2047/2048 accept boundary, and two fixture mutations narrow a stored trace index and must fail the corresponding trace assertion. A separate oversized-report control requires the explicit rejection of a 2099-byte message. A disabled probe must not panic or touch either window, while an MMIO-map failure and a conflicting diagnostic argument must panic without touching either window; a stuck AHB or APPS branch must panic with clocks=0 and the GCC state while issuing zero SDHCI accesses; a verified-clock version failure control must report clocks=1 and sample each diagnostic register in the report \(MCI version, power, clock, HOST\_CONTROL, HOST\_CONTROL2, vendor, vendor spec 3, DLL config/status, DDR config and power status\), with MCI version read twice in total \(init plus report\) and the others once. A verified-clock RCG timeout control programs the 400 kHz RCG and must report the formatted cfg=00002017, mnd=00000001/000000fc/000000fb, cmd=00000001 and branch/reset values, while reading the diagnostic-only GCC M/N/D/BCR offsets exactly once each; a clock-stability timeout control keeps the RCG update released and must report cmd=00000000 and clk=0001. A stage-3 CMD2 CRC scenario drives CMD0 and CMD1 to completion, fails CMD2 with a synthetic R2 response, and checks the fail/rsp/cmd1 fields, the exact CMD0/CMD1/CMD2 order and arguments, the report read counts and the exact CMD2 encoding. The CMD3 no-CC and late-CC scenarios run identification far enough to record a real CMD3 poll history and timeout capture, including a completion that arrives only after the poll loop expires; the CMD9 timeout scenario checks that the timeout capture is labelled CMD9 while the poll history remains CMD3's; the successful identification scenario completes through EXT\_CSD and checks that the success report carries the CMD2, pre-CMD3, poll-history, counter-frequency and CID fields and invents no timeout capture. Two full-probe failure scenarios fail the first and second CMD17 with a data CRC and a data timeout and check the block-read failure report and command stop. Fourteen mutations \(unguarded SDHCI read, removed guard, suppressed GCC report read, suppressed SDHCI report read, suppressed raw R2 snapshot, suppressed final-CMD1 window, suppressed pre-CMD2 window, suppressed CMD2 error, changed capture-format identifier, removed HOST\_VERSION sample, suppressed success-report CID, suppressed success-report poll history and suppressed timeout command index\), plus six CMD7 mutations \(suppressed pre-command validity, poll OR mask, TC validity, CC validity, trace overflow count or terminal response window\), are rejected for the expected reported reason. The stage-A additions cover the GPT gate, the 21-sector array content phase, the first-usable and last-sector reads, and four loop mutation controls \(duplicate LBA, continued error, block-count off-by-one, wrong last LBA\).](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-probe.py)

The pre-flash N8.3 p69 7c22e499 was backed up and verified as boot-before-n8.5.img.xz \(e5b43228\); full p69 readback 9d51fe80 matches candidate prefix b4f4f1ea plus unchanged tail a28b4ea2, and RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs are unchanged. A device disconnect after the backup and before any write was handled by restarting adb, re-reading p69 unchanged and writing once. The host did not reboot. One owner-classified first cold boot then returned the clean mode-3 report and TWRP: whole read-only media \(base=0, size=31268536320, GUID\_partition\_scheme, writable=0\), LBA 1 baseline match \(sum=2228245566 same=1\), exact two- and three-block reads through two-sector CMD18 chunks with clean CMD12 stops and a CMD17 odd tail, and PARTOBS samples=8 first=39 final=80 final\_ms=350 changes=2 stable=1 stable\_ms=250 deadline\_ms=4000 timed\_out=0 none=0 error=0 slots=84 reported=3 complete=0. The first sample was already 39; the intermediate count is unknown. stable=1 is a count-held window, not completeness; final=80 is consistent with the validated GPT's 80 populated entries among 84 slots, but a count is not an exact GPT-to-IOMedia comparison and complete=0 remains explicit. The boot budget is consumed, no driver fix is indicated, and initialization reliability remains unresolved. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n8.5-hardware-evidence/observation-1/ \(raw transcript 240ab0dd, interpretation 0bea53a6, observation record 92852a40\) with a separate non-self-hashing manifest; the original flash record and manifest are unchanged. The N9.1 design is docs/emmc-n9.1-gpt-iomedia-comparison-design-2026-11-07.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n8.5-hold-fixture-candidate-2026-11-07.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N9.2 working tree on this base revision\): N9.2 implements the opt-in gta4l-emmc-partcmp=1 exact GPT-to-IOMedia comparator on the host without changing the legacy mode-1/mode-3 report when the token is absent. The selector requires exactly one block token 1 or 3 with value exactly 1 and refuses a duplicate, an orphan, a wrong value or a partobs+partcmp combination with the existing IOLog wording. The comparator validates the protective MBR, the primary and backup GPT headers and entry arrays \(signature, revision, size, reserved field, CRC, geometry, reciprocal locations, usable bounds, populated-entry semantics, overlap and duplicate rules and cross-copy agreement\), normalizes populated entries to sorted \(base\_bytes, size\_bytes\) tuples and compares the exact multiset with the direct children of the selected whole-media IOGUIDPartitionScheme while excluding nested containers. Sampling takes a first snapshot at t=0 and then every 50 ms to the 2000 ms deadline; an unchanged 250 ms window yields stable=1, a stable GPT-equal set yields match=1 and complete=1, a stable unequal set stays a mismatch, a stable zero or partial set is a mismatch rather than a timeout or card failure, a changing final window yields stable=0/timed\_out=1/reason=12, cancellation returns through the no-panic path with no partial claim, the first I/O failure stops with fails=1/reason=8 and no retry, and the watchdog terminal is never overwritten or panicked twice. The PARTMAP line emits only bounded counts, stage flags and reason codes \(gpt pmbr hdr arr bak xcheck slots used media match missing extra dup nested prop samples changes stable stable\_ms deadline\_ms timed\_out budget reads blocks fails cancel complete\); the worst-case device-bounded line is 288 bytes against the 384-byte cap and the full report is 1088 bytes against the 2048-byte buffer, and a deliberately short buffer takes the existing format-failure path with empty output rather than truncating. The diagnostic payload builder maps emmc-block-1-partcmp and emmc-block-3-partcmp to the block token plus gta4l-emmc-partcmp=1, requires the fs-timer-hold manifest and refuses the partobs+partcmp combination; the checker enforces the token rules and the packager accepts both names. Host results: core 48 scenarios/38 mutation controls, adapter 85 scenarios \(21 partcmp\) with 16 adapter mutation controls \(four partcmp\) and eight policy mutation controls \(five partcmp\), 28 diagnostic payload checks including both partcmp personalities, 11 packager checks, and the evidence-count and RAM-root recipe checks pass. No candidate build, QEMU run, device access, flash or boot; N8.5's complete=0 is not upgraded and N9.3/N9.4 remain separately gated. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.57 and docs/emmc-n9.1-gpt-iomedia-comparison-design-2026-11-07.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

- `source`: [Records the N9.4 BOOT-only flash and one owner-controlled cold boot of the N9.3 exact-comparator candidate: BOOT fa55ef61 \(85,688,320 bytes, 18/18 gate\), payload fd73b37f, prelinked kernel fd2c8c65, packaged kernel d5a98930, source 062ee8fa, post-flash p69 8d5042d3 with unchanged tail a28b4ea2 and unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs. The report returned rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 but PARTMAP gpt=0 reason=10 prop=0 samples=0 reads=0 blocks=0 fails=0 cancel=0 complete=0, so whole-media discovery failed before GPT validation or comparison; MEDIA whole=0 is consistent with failure before selection, not proof of absence. The owner returned to TWRP and the boot budget is consumed. Source-grounded cause: the pinned IOStorageFamily 67f9003c whole media publishes Whole, Size, Content and Writable but no Base, while findExactWholeMedia required Base=0; kIOMediaBaseKey is set only on partition media. The host seam had set Base on every media and masked the defect. The record bounds the interpretation, preserves the owner's subjective delay observation, proposes an owner-gated absent-Base correction, and makes no completeness or reliability claim. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n9-hardware-evidence/observation-1/ \(raw 97cc0002, interpretation 75f7bb79, record ac7dcfb3\).](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.4-partcmp-observation-2026-11-07.md)

The adapter suite passes 86 scenarios with 16 adapter and eight policy mutation controls; the core suite is unchanged at 48 scenarios/38 mutation controls.
