The report returned rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 and selected the real whole media \(whole=1 base=0 size=31268536320 content=GUID\_partition\_scheme writable=0\), confirming the absent-Base correction on hardware, but PARTMAP gpt=0 reason=10 prop=0 samples=0 reads=0 blocks=0 fails=0 cancel=0 complete=0, so the GUID-scheme lookup stopped before any GPT read or comparison. Aggregate DRV 10 reads/29 blocks fails=0 max=8 and XFER req=29 c17=7 c18=11 c18b=22 cfail=0 stops=11 sfail=0 are incidental scan requests. The supplied panic suffix @Gta41EmmcBlock.cpp:1991 preserves its spelling and is the intentional report stop. The owner returned to TWRP; the one-boot budget is consumed and no completeness or reliability claim is made. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n9.6-hardware-evidence/observation-1/ \(raw 440b0d44, interpretation 57221ca8, record 87c45795\) with a separate non-self-hashing manifest binding evidence-sha256.txt 423923e7.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.6-partcmp-observation-2026-11-07.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N9.7 working tree on this base revision\): the host seam now models registration visibility for the GUID-scheme lookup. getMatchingServices returns only schemes whose registerService\(\) was called, and the fixture separates attach-only from attached-and-registered. partcmp-scheme-unregistered and partcmp-scheme-absent pin the hardware-identical gpt=0 reason=10 samples=0 reads=0 blocks=0 fails=0 cancel=0 complete=0 signature; partcmp-scheme-no-uuid, partcmp-scheme-wrong-provider and partcmp-scheme-duplicate cover the remaining predicates, and partcmp-open-refused pins the alternative whole-media open-refusal signature. The block suite passes 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 93 adapter scenarios with 16 adapter and eight policy mutation controls. Source-grounded cause: the pinned IOGUIDPartitionScheme attaches to the whole media and sets UUID during probe but never registers the scheme object, so matched-state matching cannot see it; the pinned IOMedia handleOpen sums reader levels and admits a second reader, so open refusal is not supported. The proposed smallest correction for owner approval is to enumerate the selected whole media's direct service-plane children while keeping the class, provider, UUID and exactly-one guards. No driver correction, candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, commit or push was performed.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N9.7 correction working tree on this base revision\): the owner-approved host-only N9.7 correction makes findGuidScheme\(\) enumerate the selected whole media's direct service-plane children with whole-&gt;getChildIterator\(gIOServicePlane\) instead of getMatchingServices\(kIOServiceMatchedState\); the direct-provider, UUID and exactly-one guards and the existing discovery wait are unchanged, and the four remaining matching walks are media-only. The class guard now matches the registered class by name with object-&gt;metaCast\("IOGUIDPartitionScheme"\); a direct OSDynamicCast failed the kernel link with an undefined IOGUIDPartitionScheme::metaClass because the pinned metaclass is not exported. The host seam now tracks direct children, exposes gIOServicePlane, models IOGUIDPartitionScheme, and attaches an unregistered scheme as a child without registering it. partcmp-scheme-unregistered is inverted to require the full successful comparison \(gpt=1 reason=0 slots=84 used=80 media=80 match=1 complete=1\) and asserts the scheme never registered; the new partcmp-scheme-wrong-class control rejects a plain IOService child with a UUID; partcmp-scheme-absent, partcmp-scheme-no-uuid, partcmp-scheme-wrong-provider, partcmp-scheme-duplicate and partcmp-open-refused are retained. Adapter mutation controls grow to 18 with the class-guard and exactly-one regressions; policy controls stay at eight. The block suite passes 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 94 adapter scenarios/18 adapter and eight policy mutation controls; diagnostic payload 28, packager 11, evidence-count core 187/115 and probe 196/100, RAM-root recipe and board checks pass. The corrected candidate production is recorded in the next entry; the correction is not hardware-verified and N9.6 complete=0 is not upgraded. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.64-5.3.65.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

- `source`: [Records the N9.7 BOOT-only flash and one owner-controlled cold boot of the corrected GUID-scheme exact-comparator candidate: BOOT 74a4c687 \(85,688,320 bytes, 18/18 gate\), payload 79c83d67, prelinked kernel 06826fc5, packaged kernel 61b9cea0, source 0bc83206, post-flash p69 aaaf8f03 with unchanged tail a28b4ea2 and unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs; rollback boot-before-n9.7.img.xz f7e5ac3c to raw 9c5bcf2c was not restored. The report returned rc=0 stop=0 mode=3, selected the real whole media \(whole=1 base=0 size=31268536320 content=GUID\_partition\_scheme writable=0\) and found the attached GUID scheme, but the first comparator LBA 0/512-byte read failed with gpt=0 reason=8 reads=1 blocks=1 fails=1 samples=0 complete=0. The report discards the raw IOReturn and actual bytes, so the cause is unresolved; the host-only analysis points to a page-straddling partmapPmbr buffer \(page offset 3,608 in a page-aligned 16 KiB kalloc element\) and the advertised one-segment limit producing breakSize=488 and kIOReturnDMAError in breakUpRequest before the adapter, while physical adjacency and allocation failure remain unresolved inputs. DRV online=1/wp=1/9 reads/29 blocks/fails=0/max=8 and XFER req=29/c17=7/c18=11/c18b=22/cfail=0/stops=11/sfail=0 are incidental scan requests. The owner returned to TWRP; the boot budget is consumed, N9.6 complete=0 is not upgraded and no partition-completeness or reliability claim is made. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n9.7-hardware-evidence/observation-1/ \(raw bb984f90, interpretation b38255c7, record 21a39d86\) with a separate non-self-hashing manifest binding evidence-sha256.txt c35f25d3. Findings: docs/emmc-n9.7-partcmp-observation-2026-11-07.md and docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.66.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.7-partcmp-observation-2026-11-07.md)
- `source`: [Section 5.3.66 records the N9.7 corrected-candidate observation and page-straddle analysis: BOOT-only p69 flash of 74a4c687 with readback aaaf8f03 and unchanged protected partitions, one owner-controlled cold boot, successful whole-media selection and GUID-scheme lookup, and a first comparator LBA 0/512-byte read failure with gpt=0 reason=8 reads=1 blocks=1 fails=1 samples=0 complete=0. The host-only analysis pins partmapPmbr at offset 0x0e18 in the 13,912-byte object, the page-multiple 16 KiB kalloc class with a page-aligned element base, a 488-byte first physical segment and a 24-byte second segment, and kIOReturnDMAError from breakUpRequest when the advertised one-segment limit prevents folding; physical adjacency and allocation failure remain unselected inputs. No driver correction, candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, retry, reset/repair, commit or push; N9.6 complete=0 is not upgraded.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-driver.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N9.7 read-detail working tree on this base revision\): the host-only N9.7 read-detail batch adds bounded comparator read diagnostics without changing transport policy. The report struct carries partmap\_read\_state \(0 not attempted, 1 in flight, 2 returned error, 3 short transfer, 4 full completion\), partmap\_read\_lba, partmap\_read\_blocks, partmap\_read\_bytes, the raw signed 32-bit partmap\_read\_status, the returned UInt64 partmap\_read\_actual, and the IOMemoryDescriptor length/direction; readPartmapSectors\(\) publishes the request under reportLock before media-&gt;read, latches the outcome only for the same LBA/block pair, preserves the first error or short transfer, and emits the new GTA4L-EMMC P5 READDETAIL line inside the existing 2048-byte report with a 256-byte line cap. The line is present in every PARTMAP report and is all zeros when no read was attempted. Host coverage pins pre-device descriptor-allocation refusal and open refusal \(state=0, reason=10\), a returned kIOReturnDMAError \(state=2, status=3758097098, actual=0; historical constant note: 0xe00002ca/3758097098 is kIOReturnIOError, while kIOReturnDMAError is 0xe00002d4/3758097108\), actual=0 and 511 for a 512-byte request and 512 of 1024 requested bytes \(state=3\), a short transfer after earlier successes, the in-flight watchdog snapshot \(state=1\), and full completion \(state=4\); five adapter mutation controls reject a discarded status, actual, descriptor length, short state or in-flight state, and the existing scheme class/registration/provider/UUID/exactly-one/open-refusal, comparator bounds, deadline/cancellation and legacy/partobs controls are retained. Newly executed results: block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 97 adapter scenarios with 23 adapter and eight policy mutation controls. The host seam calls doAsyncReadWrite directly and does not qualify the real context/deblock/breakup layers, so the hardware reason=8 cause remains unresolved; the page-straddle hypothesis and allocation failure stay unselected. No driver correction, candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, retry, reset/repair, commit or push; N9.6/N9.7 complete=0 are not upgraded. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.67.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)
  Command: <code>python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py</code>. Revision: `31e719fe767340c40210db1fdb21dd8baff9d5c5`.

- `source`: [Section 5.3.69 records the N9.8 read-detail observation and DMA-error exit trace: BOOT-only p69 flash of 1e767155 with readback cf13acfc and unchanged protected partitions, one owner-controlled cold boot returning rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 with the real whole media selected and the attached GUID scheme found, and a first comparator LBA 0/512-byte read failure with gpt=0 reason=8 reads=1 blocks=1 fails=1 samples=0 complete=0 plus READDETAIL state=2 lba=0 blocks=1 bytes=512 status=3758097108 actual=0 desc\_len=512 desc\_dir=1. The raw status is 0xe00002d4 = kIOReturnDMAError; the earlier 0xe00002ca/3758097098 records are kIOReturnIOError and are retained with correction notes. The host trace selects IOBlockStorageDriver::breakUpRequest breakSize==0 as the only pre-adapter DMA-error exit under the advertised one-segment limit; allocation failures return kIOReturnNoMemory; the DWARF on the flashed object keeps partmapPmbr at 0x0e18 \(3,608\) and the 488+24 page split, while physical adjacency remains unmeasured. Host suites pass 48 core/38 mutation and 97 adapter scenarios with 23 adapter and eight policy mutation controls. The boot budget is consumed, N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0 are preserved, no driver correction is authorized, and no completeness or reliability claim is made.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-driver.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N9.9 working tree on this base revision\): the N9.9 host-only correction copies every comparator read of at most 1024 bytes through one page-aligned IOMallocAligned\(PAGE\_SIZE\) buffer and copies it to the caller buffer only after a full successful transfer; the buffer is allocated in partcmp mode and freed with IOFreeAligned, while the advertised one-segment and request limits and the READDETAIL line are unchanged. The host seam now models the worst-case IOBlockStorageDriver::breakUpRequest constraint \(one segment per page, IOTrunc to the 512-byte request block size, zero break size returns kIOReturnDMAError\). partcmp-page-aligned runs the full 25-read comparator with the model enabled \(zero rejects, zero unaligned descriptors, gpt=1 reason=0 media=80 match=1 complete=1\); partcmp-page-split calls readPartmapSectors with a buffer at page offset 3,608 and requires the exact PMBR copy; the partcmp-bounce-skip mutation reverts to the caller buffer and is rejected by that scenario. Block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 99 adapter scenarios with 24 adapter and eight policy mutation controls; evidence-count core 187/115 and probe 196/100 pass. The seam still calls doAsyncReadWrite directly, the breakup model is a host reconstruction, physical adjacency remains unmeasured, and the correction is not hardware-verified. No candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, commit or push; N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0 are preserved. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.70.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

The packaged kernel carries one PARTMAP string, three P5 REPORT strings, one READDETAIL string, one PARTOBS string, one legacy PART: string, five gta4l-emmc-partcmp and five gta4l-emmc-partobs strings and no literal complete=. Block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 99 adapter scenarios with 24 adapter and eight policy mutation controls; payload, packager, boot-image, storage-ABI, evidence-count, fixture recipe and board checks pass. No flash, boot, device access, commit or push while producing the record; the separately approved BOOT-only flash and one owner-controlled cold boot are recorded in the N9.10 observation.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.10-partcmp-page-aligned-candidate-2026-11-08.md)
- `source`: [Records the N9.10 BOOT-only p69 flash and one owner-controlled cold boot of the page-aligned exact-comparator candidate: BOOT dc79fab2 \(85,688,320 bytes, 18/18 gate\), payload 800d293d, prelinked kernel fcc61714, packaged kernel 149b1e6c, source 78144956, policy f84d1bd2, header 6c51b5b3, post-flash p69 004b9d6d \(prefix dc79fab2 plus unchanged tail a28b4ea2\), unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs and saved rollback boot-before-n9.10.img.xz 3c7e9e5f to raw cf13acfc; the flash manifest verifies. The report returned rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 and selected the real whole media with the attached GUID scheme \(whole=1 base=0 size=31268536320 content=GUID\_partition\_scheme writable=0\), validated both GPT copies \(pmbr=hdr=arr=bak=xcheck=1 slots=84 used=80\), matched all 80 media tuples \(media=80 match=1 missing=0 extra=0 dup=0 nested=0 prop=0\), held the set stable for 250 ms \(samples=9 changes=3 stable=1 timed\_out=0\) and emitted complete=1 under the source guard; the comparator performed 25 reads/45 blocks with no failure \(PMBR LBA 0, primary header LBA 1, 11 primary-array reads LBA 2-22, backup header LBA 61071359, 11 backup-array reads LBA 61071327-61071347\) and READDETAIL state=4 lba=61071347 blocks=1 bytes=512 status=0 actual=512 desc\_len=512 desc\_dir=1 is the final backup-array tail read. The N9.8 first-read kIOReturnDMAError did not recur, which supports the N9.9 page-aligned read path; the N9.8 return site and physical adjacency remain host reconstructions. This is one cold boot and a same-boot completeness result, not an initialization-reliability, mount, write or wider/faster-mode result; N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0 are preserved and initialization reliability remains \[U\]. The owner returned to TWRP, the one-boot budget is consumed, and no retry, reflash, rollback, reset/repair, commit or push was performed. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n9.10-hardware-evidence/observation-1/ \(raw 2fc677de, interpretation cacab157, record 138cbeb7\) with a separate non-self-hashing manifest 2056f59e binding evidence-sha256.txt 89f5cc24.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.10-partcmp-observation-2026-11-08.md)
- `source`: [Records the host-only N9.11 protocol proposal for a bounded unchanged-N9.10 cold-boot series: fixed BOOT dc79fab2 \(payload 800d293d, prelinked kernel fcc61714, packaged kernel 149b1e6c, requalified archive 0575c413, source 78144956, policy f84d1bd2, header 6c51b5b3\), post-flash p69 004b9d6d \(prefix dc79fab2 plus unchanged tail a28b4ea2\), unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs and retained rollback 3c7e9e5f to raw cf13acfc; read-only pre-series and post-series p69/protected-partition verification; an owner-selected finite budget of additional owner-controlled full power-off cold boots \(three additional proposed, four observations total including N9.10 observation 1\); per-attempt raw report capture and TWRP-return confirmation; outcome classes A \(no report/pre-report failure, not a pass\), B \(initialization/setup failure with FAIL valid=1\), C \(comparator complete=0\), D \(repeat complete=1 with the expected deterministic PARTMAP/READDETAIL signature\) and E \(malformed/partial report\); stop at the first A/B/C/E or identity/partition/TWRP deviation; and no reflash, rebuild, rollback, reset/repair, writes, host reboot, retry, extra boot, commit or push. The series is descriptive, not a failure-rate estimate, does not close initialization reliability, and preserves N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0 and \[U\]. No device access, flash, boot or commit was performed while writing the proposal.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.11-reliability-protocol-2026-11-08.md)
- `source`: [Records the owner-reported N9.11 repeatability series on the unchanged N9.10 image: after the captured observation-1 cold boot \(complete=1 with the full P5 report\), the owner reports three additional cold boots and three warm boots, every one showing complete=1, with no photos or full reports, no intervening flash and a return to TWRP. Totals are 4 cold and 3 warm boots since the flash; the three warm boots are outside the written N9.11 cold-boot protocol. Only observation-1 is captured; the six additional outcomes are owner-reported, no pre-series or post-series p69/protected-partition verification was performed, and no running-image hash or boot timestamp was measured. The series deviates from the protocol's per-attempt capture and hash-chained observation requirements. It supports descriptive repeatability of the owner-observed same-boot completeness outcome on this image; it does not verify the other report fields for the uncaptured boots, does not establish initialization reliability, a failure rate or a statistical bound, and does not qualify mount, arbitrary I/O, writes or wider/faster modes. N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0 are preserved and initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n9.10-hardware-evidence/reliability-series/ \(raw 141021e2, interpretation 6552f2a8, record e0fbe0c8\) with a separate non-self-hashing manifest 76c45042 binding the flash manifest 89f5cc24 and the observation-1 manifest 2056f59e. No device access, flash, boot or commit was performed while recording the series.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n9.11-reliability-observation-2026-11-08.md)

Candidate: prelinked kernel 12e00d9f \(51,277,824 bytes; plan 83a8b81b\), kernel-build archive b137c2e4 with Gta4lEmmcBlock.c 9dad3d79 to Gta4lEmmcBlock.o 2402c895 and Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp 6a11aa76 to Gta4lEmmcBlockCpp.o 5f72925e, candidate storage-ABI audit out/gta4l/storage-abi-12e00d9f \(gdb-stop 275d693e, interface-audit 48d8d4a7, provenance 99c15186, run b9888e50; 190 slots, 181 bindings, 26 overrides, 0 unresolved imports, runtime\_qualified=false\), requalified archive 5a59d055 with byte-identical members, packaged kernel 05fde642, payload e2dc899d \(83,886,080 bytes, manifest 08ff16bf, 18/18 contract, emmc-block-3-partread to gta4l-emmc-block=3 gta4l-emmc-partread=1 with the fs-timer-hold fixture\) and BOOT image 0cee22c9 \(85,688,320 bytes, manifest 31ce0993, 18/18 gate, only the ramdisk differs from the ce4d76e8 v5 baseline\). The six digests were re-verified against the retained recovery readbacks, primary/backup GPT prefixes and the boot-tail ranges of boot-backup-20261027-pmgr.img; the retained GPT parses as 128 entries of 84 bytes with backup LBA 61071359, usable bounds 34/61071326 and the boot entry at 2255360 with 196608 sectors. The packaged kernel carries one PARTREAD, one READTEST, one READFAIL, one PARTMAP, three REPORT, one READDETAIL, one PARTOBS, one legacy PART: and six gta4l-emmc-partread strings and no literal complete=. Block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 114 adapter scenarios with 33 adapter and eight policy mutation controls; payload, packager, boot-image, storage-ABI, evidence-count, fixture recipe and board checks pass. No flash, boot, device access, commit or push while producing the record; the separately approved BOOT-only flash and one owner-reported cold boot were executed later and are recorded in the next entry, and they qualify only the six pinned ranges on that boot, not arbitrary I/O, mount, writes, wider/faster modes or initialization reliability. - `source`: [Records the host-only N10.1 captured repeatability protocol proposal for the unchanged already-flashed N10.1 image: fixed BOOT 0cee22c9 \(payload e2dc899d, prelinked kernel 12e00d9f, packaged kernel 05fde642, requalified archive 5a59d055, source 6a11aa76, policy 9dad3d79, header d05b982e\), post-flash p69 cc3c8492 \(prefix 0cee22c9 plus unchanged tail a28b4ea2\), unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs and retained rollback de7d4d51 to raw 004b9d6d; read-only pre-series and post-series p69/protected-partition verification; an owner-selected finite budget of additional owner-controlled full power-off cold boots \(three additional proposed, four observations total including N10.1 observation 1\); per-attempt full P5 report capture including PARTREAD/READTEST/READFAIL and TWRP-return confirmation; outcome classes A \(no report/pre-report failure, not a pass\), B \(initialization/setup failure with FAIL valid=1\), C \(PARTREAD complete=0 or failed/read/budget/cancel/media/part failure\), D \(repeat complete=1 with the expected deterministic PARTREAD/READTEST signature\) and E \(malformed/partial report\); stop at the first A/B/C/E or identity/partition/TWRP deviation; and no reflash, rebuild, rollback, reset/repair, writes, host reboot, retry, extra boot, commit or push. The series is descriptive, not a failure-rate estimate, does not close initialization reliability, and preserves N8.5/N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0, N9.10 complete=1, the N9.11 owner-reported series and N10.1 observation 1; initialization reliability remains \[U\]. No device access, flash, boot or commit was performed while writing the proposal.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.1-partread-repeatability-protocol-2026-11-08.md)

The approved read-only pre-series and post-series checks matched p69 full/prefix/tail and unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs. Together with observation 1, four captured cold-boot observations show descriptive repeatability of the six pinned content matches only; the series does not establish initialization reliability, a failure rate, arbitrary I/O, mount, writes, wider/faster modes, DMA/CQE/ICE or the real context/deblock/breakup layers, and preserves N8.5/N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0, the N9.10 comparator result and N10.1 observation 1; initialization reliability remains \[U\]. The three-boot budget is consumed and no further boot, commit or push was performed.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.1-partread-repeatability-observation-2026-11-08.md)

- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N10.8 working tree on this base revision\): N10.8 implements the owner-approved host-only deterministic gta4l-emmc-breakup=1 qualification without changing the absent-token legacy, partobs, partcmp, partread or deblock reports. The selector requires exactly one block token 1/3 with value exactly 1 and refuses a duplicate, an orphan, a malformed value and every partobs/partcmp/partread/deblock combination. The mode-scoped advertisement is 8 read blocks/4096 bytes/3 segments only for breakup; the other modes keep 64/32768 and their existing segment counts. Four pinned boot-partition cases \(4096/4096, 4096/65536, 4096/65024 and 4196/65024\) cover the direct executeRequest short-circuit, an exact 16-stage split, a 15-stage split with a 7-block final stage and the unaligned split through the deblocker's three-chunk descriptor, each with a full pinned SHA-256 printed as a 16-byte prefix. One page-aligned 65536-byte IOMallocAligned bounce buffer is allocated only in breakup mode and freed with IOFreeAligned. The report thread records the active case under reportLock before media-&gt;read and clears it after the call without holding the lock across the call; doAsyncReadWrite calls the mode-scoped observer after the null/zero checks and before the controller gate, and the observer takes reportLock briefly, classifies tagged direct-path and tag-0 deblocker-path subrequests, records subrequest count/blocks/max/order, counts unattributable requests as foreign, retains no buffer and performs no I/O. The first failure latches case index/reason/status/actual/decomposition/digest prefix, later read/short/hash/descriptor/split/order failures continue, media/partition/open/budget/cancellation stop the run, and complete=1 requires all four cases plus reason=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 foreign=0 limit=8 seg=3. Worst case is 20000+6000+2000+1000=29000 ms below the 30000 ms watchdog; BREAKUP stays within 448 bytes, BREAKUPREAD within 320 bytes and the whole report within 2048 bytes with fail-closed formatting. The expected full-pass line is cases=4 passed=4 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1 reads=4 bytes=199680 span=200192 blocks=391 subrequests=49 max=8 foreign=0 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 limit=8 seg=3 cap=61071360. Host results: block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 178 adapter scenarios with 58 adapter, three support and eight policy mutation controls, covering the full pass, production-limit control, seg=1 anti-weakening, wrong decomposition, foreign traffic, read/short/hash/descriptor/range/zero-length failures, budget, cancellation, media/partition/open failures, watchdog race and selector/property/report controls; diagnostic payload 37 checks and packager 14 checks including both breakup personalities, the fs-timer-hold requirement and exclusivity refusals. The host model reconstructs the IOBreaker/IODeblocker split and does not execute the real family code. No candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, commit or push; N10.9/N10.10 remain separately gated, N10.6 remains the only breakup-layer hardware observation and initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.82 and docs/emmc-n10.7-breakup-qualification-design-2026-11-09.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

- `source`: [Records the N10.10 BOOT-only flash and one owner-reported cold boot of the N10.9 deterministic breakup candidate: BOOT a8f4a54b \(85688320 bytes\), payload 706cfd53, prelinked kernel 71828829, packaged kernel a8a5a609, archive 8e636a01, post-flash p69 4b51ff21 \(candidate prefix a8f4a54b plus unchanged tail a28b4ea2\), unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs, rollback boot-before-n10.10.img.xz 70312a39 to raw f4882047. The report returned rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 with the read-only whole media and BREAKUP cases=4 passed=4 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1 reads=4 bytes=199680 span=200192 blocks=391 subrequests=49 max=8 foreign=0 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 limit=8 seg=3 cap=61071360; BREAKUPREAD index=3 target=1 start=4196 length=65024 span=65536 actual=65024 status=0 subrequests=16 blocks=128 max=8 digest=bc0b9bb9c6dd11c3aea665d01dc5f108, the first 16 bytes of the pinned case-3 digest bc0b9bb9...26db17. Every pinned digest matched, media=0 part=0 are clear failure flags, and complete=1 is the guard at package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp:3559-3572. The whole-boot counters DRV reads=58 blocks=420 fails=0 max=8 and XFER req=420 c17=8 c18=206 c18b=412 stops=206 sfail=0 show the mode-scoped eight-block cap and 49 driver-level subrequests, so the real IOBreaker/breakUpRequest split path was entered; the N10.6 deblock-mode 24-block three-segment fold remains a distinct observation. No post-boot readback was taken; the report is bound by the pre-boot post-flash readback, target-binding re-verification and pinned digest. This qualifies only the four pinned boot-partition ranges and the real breakup path on one cold boot, not arbitrary I/O, mount, writes, wider/faster modes, DMA/CQE/ICE or initialization reliability; N8.5/N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0, the N9.10 comparator result, the N9.11 owner-reported series, the N10.1/N10.2 partread results and the N10.6 deblock result are preserved. The owner returned to TWRP, the one-boot budget is consumed, and no retry, reflash, rollback, reset/repair, mount, commit or push was performed. Raw evidence is in out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-n10.9-hardware-evidence/ \(transcript 51482570, flash record 26949764, manifest 35cb4a3a\). Findings: docs/emmc-n10.10-breakup-hardware-result-2026-11-09.md and docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.84.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.10-breakup-hardware-result-2026-11-09.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N10.12 working tree on this base revision\): N10.12 implements the owner-approved host-only bounded aligned read sweep from the N10.11 design without changing the absent-token legacy, partobs, partcmp, partread, deblock or breakup reports. The gta4l-emmc-sweep=1 selector requires exactly one block token 1/3 with value exactly 1 and refuses a duplicate, an orphan, a malformed value and every partobs/partcmp/partread/deblock/breakup combination. The mode-scoped advertisement is 8 read blocks/4096 bytes/one segment only for sweep. Six cases: two pinned boot anchors \(4096/4096 and 4096/16384\) with pinned SHA-256 prefixes and four runtime windows derived from the discovered 196608-block boot partition \(32 blocks at start block 51528, 16 at 102928, 8 at 154296 and 8 at 9128\) using \(\(i+1\)\*104729\) % \(\(P-W\)/8\) \* 8; the whole table is validated before any case I/O and a different P is refused. Every case is read through the real media path into one page-aligned 16384-byte IOMallocAligned buffer and through a direct per-block CMD17 reference into a second page-aligned 16384-byte buffer, both freed with IOFreeAligned, then compared byte-for-byte with the anchor digest check. The report thread arms the active case under reportLock before media-&gt;read and clears it after; doAsyncReadWrite calls the mode-scoped observer after the null/zero checks and before the controller gate, and the observer takes reportLock briefly, classifies tagged and tag-0 range subrequests, records count/blocks/max/order, counts unattributable requests as foreign, retains no buffer and performs no I/O. The first failure latches index/reason/status/actual/decomposition/reference status/bytes/mismatch offset/digest prefixes; later read/short/mismatch/descriptor/split/order failures continue, while media/partition/open/budget/cancel/reference/anchor failures stop; complete=1 requires all six cases plus reason=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 foreign=0 anchor=1 mismatches=0 limit=8 seg=1. Reason 14 is the reference/media mismatch and 15 a reference failure. The worst case is 20000+2000+4000+1700=27700 ms below the 30000 ms watchdog; SWEEP stays within 384 bytes, SWEEPREAD within 352 bytes and the whole report within 2048 bytes. The expected full-pass line is cases=6 passed=6 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1 reads=6 bytes=53248 span=53248 blocks=104 subrequests=13 max=8 foreign=0 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 refs=6 refbytes=53248 anchor=1 mismatches=0 limit=8 seg=1 cap=61071360. Host results: block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 218 adapter scenarios with 71 adapter, three support and eight policy mutation controls; diagnostic payload 40 checks and packager 15 checks including both sweep personalities, the fs-timer-hold requirement and the exclusivity refusals; core 187/115 and probe 196/100; evidence-count, fixture recipe, board and TOML checks pass. The host model reconstructs the media/breaker path and does not execute the real family code. No candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, commit or push; N10.13/N10.14 remain separately gated, the sweep has no hardware observation, N10.10 remains the only breakup-layer hardware result and initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.85 and docs/emmc-n10.11-bounded-read-sweep-design-2026-11-09.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)
  Command: <code>python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py; python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-diagnostic-payload.py; python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-diagnostic-package.py; python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-evidence-counts.py</code>. Revision: `1705061b5bf219cbfede83b4091261d51edb5817`.

- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N10.15 working tree on this base revision\): N10.15 implements the owner-approved host-only read-only consumer from the N10.15 design without changing the absent-token legacy, partobs, partcmp, partread, deblock, breakup or sweep reports. The gta4l-emmc-consume=1 selector requires exactly one block token 3 and refuses block 1, a duplicate, an orphan, a non-1 or malformed value and every partobs/partcmp/partread/deblock/breakup/sweep combination. The mode keeps the production 64-block/32768-byte/one-segment advertisement; a distinct Gta4lEmmcConsumer IOService client opens the pinned BOOT partition and reads one 1024-byte window at offset 0 through one IOMallocContiguous\(32768, PAGE\_SIZE, nullptr\) buffer and a withAddress\(buffer, 1024, kIODirectionIn\)+kIOMemoryAutoPrepare descriptor whose getPhysicalSegment\(0\) run is validated to at least 1024 bytes; the buffer is freed with IOFreeContiguous on every path and no fragmented fallback or retry exists. The entry observer at the top of doAsyncReadWrite\(\) runs before the gate and requires the exact nonzero tag, whole-media first block, block count and range; tag-0 or any other request is foreign, a duplicate or missing/extra case is ORDER, and the completion side credits the private block.counters deltas under the gate \(one two-block CMD18 chunk, two blocks, one stop\). Option A prints the observed first-16-byte SHA-256 prefix with expected=00000000000000000000000000000000 and mismatch=0 and the host recomputes the two-block fixture through gta4l\_test\_fill\_sector\(\), so a content mismatch fails acceptance host-side without changing the on-device complete field. The expected success lines are CONSUME: cases=1 passed=1 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1 reads=1 bytes=1024 span=1024 blocks=2 subrequests=1 max=2 foreign=0 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 open=1 close=1 alloc=0 client=1 mismatches=0 limit=64 seg=1 cap=61071360 and CONSUMEREAD: index=0 target=1 start=0 length=1024 span=1024 actual=1024 status=0 subrequests=1 blocks=2 max=2 digest=&lt;observed&gt; expected=00000000000000000000000000000000 mismatch=0; CONSUMEFAIL carries reason, case, target, start, length, span, requested, completed, actual, status, digest, expected and mismatch. Consume mode suppresses READ/QUAL/QUAL3, the three line caps are 320/352/384 bytes and the pinned worst report is 1959 content bytes inside 2048. The worst case is the conditional 20000+2000+3000=25000 ms against the 30000 ms watchdog. Host results: block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 248 adapter scenarios with 95 adapter/support and 10 policy mutation controls, including the full pass, production advertisement, one-segment descriptor, digest host comparison, media/partition/open/alloc/descriptor failures, short/read/cancel/budget/foreign/order controls, suppression and width controls and legacy equality; diagnostic payload 42 checks and packager 16 checks including the emmc-block-3-consume personality, the fs-timer-hold fixture requirement, the block-1 refusal and the six-mode exclusivity refusals; builder and checker self-tests pass. No candidate build, ABI/QEMU audit, device access, flash, boot, commit or push; N10.15 has no hardware observation, the timing argument remains conditional and initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.88 and docs/emmc-n10.15-read-only-consumer-design-2026-11-10.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

Revision: `dfb7d5475a70f61d3272862995a829b9d31020b5`.

Candidate: prelinked kernel d943c228 \(51437568 bytes; plan 29b7a386; original f18b4df8\), kernel-build archive 12d851a8 with Gta4lEmmcBlock.c 9587c98a to Gta4lEmmcBlock.o cd224a74 and Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp 86dab8b1 to Gta4lEmmcBlockCpp.o eff8fd7c, candidate storage-ABI audit out/gta4l/storage-abi-d943c228 \(gdb-stop 92ccffbf, interface-audit fdfae103, provenance a9088e50, run 615b27d2; 190 slots, 181 bindings, 26 overrides, 0 unresolved imports, vtable 0xfffffff005cb44c0, metaclass 0xfffffff006add1d0, service metaclass 0xfffffff007b175b8, bsd\_init 0xfffffff0076efa3c, runtime\_qualified=false\), requalified archive 12d851a8 with byte-identical members, packaged kernel e5ba92e3, payload e05eb1cb \(83886080 bytes, manifest c23aaa9b, 45/45 contract, emmc-block-3-consume to gta4l-emmc-block=3 gta4l-emmc-consume=1 with the fs-timer-hold fixture 00363681\) and BOOT image 777b446c \(85688320 bytes, manifest 09dfe4ea, gate 22587d59, 18/18, only the ramdisk differs from the ce4d76e8 v5 baseline, header ID 4b15eb2a\). The external window reference was rebound from the N10.13 image's 5a25ed5e to the packaged image's first 1024 bytes 97479ae8 \(prefix 97479ae876ec6fe6f45b38492b9eb9ac\), which the on-device CONSUMEREAD digest must equal; a mismatch fails acceptance host-side only. The packaged kernel carries one CONSUME, one CONSUMEREAD and one CONSUMEFAIL string, ten gta4l-emmc-consume strings and no literal complete=. Block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 248 adapter scenarios with 95 adapter/support and 10 policy mutation controls; core 187/115, probe 196/100, payload 42, packager 16, boot-image, storage-ABI, evidence-count, fixture recipe, board and TOML checks pass. The owner accepted the conditional 25000 ms timing margin for the separately approved BOOT-only flash and one cold boot. No flash, boot or device access was performed while producing the candidate; N10.15 has no hardware observation, the one-boot budget is not consumed, N10.14 remains the only sweep-mode hardware result, N10.10 remains the only breakup-layer hardware result and initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-n10.15-consume-candidate-2026-11-10.md and docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.89.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.15-consume-candidate-2026-11-10.md)

The report returned rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 with the read-only whole media and CONSUME cases=1 passed=1 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1 reads=1 bytes=1024 span=1024 blocks=2 subrequests=1 max=2 foreign=0 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0 media=0 part=0 open=1 close=1 alloc=0 client=1 mismatches=0 limit=64 seg=1 cap=61071360; CONSUMEREAD index=0 target=1 start=0 length=1024 span=1024 actual=1024 status=0 subrequests=1 blocks=2 max=2 digest=97479ae876ec6fe6f45b38492b9eb9ac expected=00000000000000000000000000000000 mismatch=0, equal to the option A external reference 97479ae8 \(the first 1024 bytes of the packaged image\). complete=1 is the guard at package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp:5209-5243, which also requires creditedC18=1, creditedC18b=2 and creditedStops=1. Consume mode suppressed READ/QUAL/QUAL3 through the report-&gt;consume == 0 guard at package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.c:2648; no CONSUMEFAIL appeared. The whole-boot counters DRV reads=10 blocks=31 fails=0 max=8 and XFER req=31 c17=7 c18=12 c18b=24 stops=12 sfail=0 show the case's one two-block CMD18 chunk and the same 29-block incidental overlay observed by the N9.6/N9.7 partcmp, N10.10 breakup and N10.14 sweep boots; DRV max=8 comes from that overlay, not the case. The supplied @Gta41EmmcBlock.cpp:5719 is the intentional terminal report stop; the owner's 35-zero expected= field is a transcription artifact. No post-boot readback was taken; the report is bound by the pre-boot post-flash readback, target-binding re-verification and the option A digest comparison. This qualifies only the one 1024-byte BOOT window on one cold boot, not arbitrary I/O, mount, writes, wider/faster modes, DMA/CQE/ICE or initialization reliability; the 25000 ms timing argument remains conditional and accepted; N8.5/N9.6/N9.7/N9.8 complete=0, the N9.10 comparator result, the N9.11 owner-reported series, the N10.1/N10.2 partread results, the N10.6 deblock result, the N10.10 breakup result and the N10.14 sweep result are preserved. The owner returned to TWRP, the one-boot budget is consumed, and no retry, reflash, rollback, reset/repair, mount, commit or push was performed. - `source`: [Records the host-only N10.16 readiness audit and the follow-on host-only width-mode implementation for the first M3 rung: a staged 4-bit SDR read through the published storage path at the existing 400 kHz. The audit maps the H15-H17 verified width-switch/recovery helpers onto the production block driver, fixes the insertion point after initializeHardware\(\) returns and before the baseline read/publication, preserves the legacy 1-bit path and the production 64-block/32768-byte/one-segment advertisement, and implements the opt-in gta4l-emmc-width=4 mode requiring exactly one block token 3 and refusing orphan, duplicate, non-4, non-3 and conflicting mode tokens. The mode reads the pinned BOOT-partition window \(device LBA 2255360, two blocks, 1024 bytes\) at 1-bit through the block core, performs the verified 4-bit switch \(pre-switch EXT\_CSD baseline, CMD6 BUS\_WIDTH, CMD13 TRAN/READY\_FOR\_DATA wait, HOST\_CONTROL apply/readback, post-switch read-only EXT\_CSD properties comparison\), re-reads the same window at 4-bit, compares all 1024 bytes, reverts to 1-bit with the baseline-based recovery helper, and reports WIDTH/WIDTHREAD/WIDTHFAIL inside the 2048-byte report; byte-183 equality is never used and CMD19/CMD14 is excluded. The success bound is 26230 ms \(2000 baseline + 3000 pre-read + 6010 switch + 2000 existing baseline read + 3000 4-bit read + 8220 revert\) and the failed-switch bound is 21030 ms against the 30000 ms watchdog; the margin remains conditional. The host-only follow-on batch also adds the emmc-block-3-width4 diagnostic personality, which requires the fs-timer-hold fixture and rejects orphan, duplicate, malformed, non-4, block-1, probe and modifier-conflict tokens. The audit verdict was GO for a host-only implementation/test batch, which now passes, and NO-GO remains for candidate production or hardware until owner CMD6-write and read-scope approval, timing acceptance, content-reference rebinding, candidate-specific ABI/packaging gates and a finite-boot-budget hardware protocol close. No candidate build, device access, flash or boot was performed; cold-boot identification reliability, repeatability and failure rates remain \[U\], and no 4-bit publication result exists yet.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.16-4bit-sdr-readiness-2026-11-10.md)
- `source`: [Records the host-only N10.16 width-mode candidate: prelinked kernel 4a7d254e \(51470336 bytes; plan 015bf7bf\), candidate-matched storage-ABI audit out/gta4l/storage-abi-4a7d254e4d73 \(190 slots, 181 bindings, 26 overrides, 0 unresolved original imports; service metaclass fffffff007b1f5b8, bsd\_init fffffff0076efa3c\), requalified block archive e0e13c93 with members matching the archive linked into the kernel, payload fafca83a \(83886080 bytes, 55/55 contract, emmc-block-3-width4 to gta4l-emmc-block=3 gta4l-emmc-width=4 with the fs-timer-hold fixture\) and BOOT image f18694b7 \(85688320 bytes, 18/18 gate, only the ramdisk differs, header ID 3326d760\). The width report is bound to the final image first 1024 bytes b29e23fda6d6a1dfc969c652a17f7ae6; scripts/check-gta4l-width4-evidence.py checks digest, predigest, match and case shape with matching/changed/missing/malformed/truncated controls. - `source`: [Records the host-only N10.16 pre-hardware review: confirms the C2 candidate artifacts, corrects the WIDTHREAD digest field to 16 bytes/32 hex characters \(prefix b29e23fda6d6a1dfc969c652a17f7ae6\), and finds a report gate-timeout gap: the report thread blocks on the controller gate while a slow 64-block in-flight request can hold it for roughly 32 two-block chunks x \(1 s inhibit + 1 s data\), so the 30,000 ms watchdog can fire before WIDTH starts. The 24,230 ms figure combines a conditional 13,010 ms pre-watchdog prefix with an 11,220 ms report budget and is not an end-to-end proof. Verdict: host-qualified candidate but NO-GO for hardware until a bounded-gate corrective batch, a source-derived pre-watchdog bound, host gate-timeout/watchdog-race/no-revert tests, and a rebuilt/rebound candidate close the gap. No device access, flash or boot; owner CMD6/read-scope/timing/budget approvals remain pending and initialization reliability remains \[U\].](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.16-prehardware-review-2026-11-11.md)
- `source`: [Records the host-only N10.16 report-gate corrective candidate: prelinked kernel 2be2eb06 \(51470336 bytes; plan f96f6ada\), candidate-matched storage-ABI audit out/gta4l/storage-abi-2be2eb0605b2 \(190 slots, 181 bindings, 26 overrides, 0 unresolved original imports; service metaclass fffffff007b1f5b8\), requalified block archive abcf935f with members matching the archive linked into the kernel, payload 10ff7ee8 \(83886080 bytes, 55/55 contract, emmc-block-3-width4\) and BOOT image 58352cd3 \(85688320 bytes, 18/18 gate, only the ramdisk differs, header ID 7b9d5907\); packaged kernel e71fa4eb. The width report is bound to the final image first 1024 bytes a6da82e356863d1ab7e31c21a8fd85fc. The corrective change adds a 2000 ms bounded gate acquisition, terminal reason 8 with media offline and no revert claim, and a no-gate terminal snapshot; the width-gate-timeout scenario covers the held-gate path. Timing is 26230 ms success \(13010 pre-watchdog + 13220 report including 2000 gate\), failed-switch 21030 ms, and the pre-watchdog path remains conditional. Host block 48/38 and 285 adapter/104/10, core 189/117, probe 196/100, payload/package, storage-ABI, fixture, inventory/board, packaging, evidence-count and TOML checks pass. No device access, flash or boot while producing the candidate; it was subsequently flashed to p69 and its one owner-approved cold boot passed the terminal complete=1 guard and restored BOOT, recorded in docs/emmc-n10.16-width4-hardware-observation-2026-11-11.md; F5/F3 and initialization reliability \[U\] are unchanged. Findings: docs/emmc-n10.16-width4-gatefix-candidate-2026-11-11.md, docs/emmc-n10.16-prehardware-review-2026-11-11.md and docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.91.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.16-width4-gatefix-candidate-2026-11-11.md)
- `source`: [Records the owner-approved one-boot N10.16 width4 hardware protocol for BOOT image 58352cd3 \(payload 10ff7ee8, kernel 2be2eb06, first-1024-byte prefix a6da82e356863d1ab7e31c21a8fd85fc\): BOOT-only write to p69, readback prefix/tail and protected-partition checks, one controlled boot, full P5 WIDTH/WIDTHREAD report capture, host digest comparison, stop-on-first-non-pass, one-boot budget consumed by any attempt, and TWRP return. No CMD19/CMD14, other width, HS/DDR, mount, user-data I/O or storage writes. Executed on 2026-11-11 via the owner-controlled TWRP host: BOOT-only p69 write of 58352cd3, post-flash readback cb157626 \(candidate prefix matches, p69 tail beyond the image unchanged a28b4ea2\) with unchanged RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs, one owner-confirmed cold boot and TWRP return. The terminal report returned rc=0 stop=0 with WIDTH cases=1 passed=1 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1, switch=4 verified=1 hb=0 ha=2 rb=0 cmd6=1, reads=2 bytes=2048 blocks=4 subrequests=2 max=2 match=1 mismatches=0 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0, revert=1 rverified=1 rhb=2 rha=0 rcmd6=1 and limit=64 seg=1 cap=61071360; WIDTHREAD digest=predigest=a6da82e356863d1ab7e31c21a8fd85fc match=1 was verified host-side against the image. rb=0 is the diagnostic EXT\_CSD byte-183 readback \(W/E\_P, not a completion condition\) and rcmd6=1 is first-attempt revert success; the terminal guard at package/Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp:6052-6091 accepts both. The written protocol's rb=1/rcmd6=2 expectations were simulated values and are corrected in the record. The pre-flash image 6fa2b7e9 was restored and verified byte-for-byte with unchanged protected partitions. The one-boot budget is consumed; no retry, extra boot, mount, write or further hardware qualification is authorized.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.16-width4-hardware-protocol-2026-11-11.md)
- `source`: [Records the N10.16 one-cold-boot width4 hardware observation: candidate BOOT 58352cd3 \(85688320 bytes; payload 10ff7ee8, kernel 2be2eb06, first-1024-byte prefix a6da82e3\), pre-flash p69 6fa2b7e9 \(prefix 97479ae8, tail d737414c\) with RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs 14e11370/51fa824c/3a265d58, BOOT-only post-flash p69 cb157626 \(candidate prefix matches, tail beyond image unchanged a28b4ea2, protected partitions unchanged\), and an owner-transcribed terminal report from one cold boot with TWRP return. The driver's terminal guard computed complete=1: WIDTH cases=1 passed=1 failed=0 reason=0, switch=4 verified=1 hb=0 ha=2 rb=0 cmd6=1, reads=2 bytes=2048 blocks=4 subrequests=2 max=2 match=1, revert=1 rverified=1 rhb=2 rha=0 rcmd6=1, limit=64 seg=1 cap=61071360; WIDTHREAD index=0 lba=2255360 length/span/actual=1024 status=0 subrequests=1 blocks=2 max=2 digest=predigest=a6da82e3 match=1, verified host-side by scripts/check-gta4l-width4-evidence.py. rb=0 is the diagnostic W/E\_P byte-183 readback and rcmd6=1 is first-attempt success; both are accepted by the guard and the protocol's earlier rb=1/rcmd6=2 values are corrected. Bounded verdict: verified 4-bit switch, byte-exact two-read comparison, verified 1-bit revert and read-only boot continuation pass on one boot; initialization reliability, repeatability, failure rate, 64-block/arbitrary I/O, writes, wider/faster modes, DMA/CQE/ICE, mount/root and 4-bit publication remain unestablished and initialization reliability stays \[U\]. The exact pre-flash p69 was restored and verified with unchanged protected partitions; evidence is in the private hardware-evidence directory. No retry, extra boot, commit or push.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.16-width4-hardware-observation-2026-11-11.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N10.16 working tree\): implements the host-only opt-in gta4l-emmc-width=4 4-bit SDR mode without changing the absent-token legacy path or the production 64-block/32768-byte/one-segment advertisement. The selector requires exactly one block token 3 and refuses an orphan, duplicate, malformed or non-4 width token, block values other than 3, and every partobs/partcmp/partread/deblock/breakup/sweep/consume combination. The mode reads the pinned BOOT window \(device LBA 2255360, two blocks\) at 1-bit through the block core, performs the verified 4-bit switch \(CMD6, CMD13 TRAN wait, HOST\_CONTROL apply/readback, read-only EXT\_CSD properties comparison; byte-183 equality is never used and CMD19/CMD14 is excluded\), re-reads the same window at 4-bit, compares all 1024 bytes, reverts to 1-bit with the captured baseline and reports WIDTH/WIDTHREAD/WIDTHFAIL inside the 2048-byte report \(line bounds 513/329/127, report bound 1772\). A 4-bit read failure clears online and preserves the first failure; a comparison mismatch or revert failure is terminal. The success bound is 26230 ms and the failed-switch bound 21030 ms against the 30000 ms watchdog, conditional. Block suite 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 285 adapter scenarios with 104 adapter/support and 10 policy mutation controls; core 189/117, probe 196/100 and the evidence-count checks pass. The diagnostic payload builder and packager suites pass with the new emmc-block-3-width4 personality \(gta4l-emmc-block=3 gta4l-emmc-width=4\), requiring the fs-timer-hold fixture and rejecting the early-panic fixture, orphan/duplicate/malformed/non-4/block-1/duplicate-block/probe/modifier-conflict tokens and width smuggling into other personalities; this host batch built no payload. The separately approved gate-fix candidate next bounds the report gate at 2000 ms and adds the width-gate-timeout case. Candidate, owner and hardware gates are tracked in the readiness document section 10. No candidate build, device access, flash or boot in this host batch; F5 remains measured PASS at 1-bit and F3 at 4-bit for one reported boot each, no 4-bit publication result exists and initialization reliability remains \[U\].](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)
  Command: <code>python3 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py; python3 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc.py; python3 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-probe.py; python3 -B scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-evidence-counts.py; python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-diagnostic-payload.py; python3.11 -B scripts/test-gta4l-diagnostic-package.py; python3.11 -B scripts/build-gta4l-diagnostic-payload.py --self-test; python3.11 -B scripts/check-gta4l-payload.py --self-test; python3.11 -B scripts/check-gta4l-width4-evidence.py --self-test</code>. Revision: `3b69bbca6e50c57276643d1127a6873b9e4cd080`.
- `source`: [Records the reconciled N10.17 design for the first sustained 4-bit SDR read rung: the opt-in gta4l-emmc-widthhold=1 selector requires exactly one gta4l-emmc-block=3 token and refuses duplicates, unsupported values and every partobs/partcmp/partread/deblock/breakup/sweep/consume/width combination while preserving the legacy and N10.16 behavior. The mode captures a 1-bit EXT\_CSD baseline and an 8-block/4096-byte 1-bit BOOT reference, performs the verified 4-bit switch at the existing 400 kHz, issues four tagged two-block reads at BOOT offsets 0, 1024, 2048 and 3072 bytes \(LBAs 2255360, 2255362, 2255364 and 2255366\), compares every byte against the reference slice and reverts to 1-bit with the captured baseline on every post-switch exit. The report family is one WIDTHHOLD summary plus one WIDTHHOLDREAD last-case line on success or one WIDTHHOLDFAIL line on failure, with legacy READ/QUAL/QUAL3 and PART suppression, line bounds 905/335/227 bytes and a 2078-byte worst report inside the 2560-byte buffer. The report-thread bound is 27720 ms \(8000 media wait + 2000 BOOT deadline + 3000 case budget + 4500 two-block case + 2000 revert gate + 8220 verified revert\) below the 30000 ms watchdog. The design resolves the reference, timing, buffer and recovery questions without weakening a guard; no implementation, candidate build, device access, flash or boot was performed.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.17-sustained-width-read-design-2026-11-11.md)
- `source`: [Records the N10.19 host-only widthhold candidate production from the N10.18 implementation \(commit 0c6aebe\): prelinked kernel 23e3b6d90f4a \(51527680 bytes; plan 144d113f\), candidate-matched storage-ABI audit out/gta4l/storage-abi-23e3b6d90f4a \(gdb-stop 275b626a, interface-audit 9963b239, provenance 00c51c63, run 268e34ca; 190 method slots, 0 unresolved methods, abstract\_complete\_destructors\_trap=true, live\_native\_4k=true\), kernel-build and requalified-tree archives byte-identical 6dcdfe9a with matching members Gta4lEmmcBlock.o bc58e01c, Gta4lEmmcBlockCpp.o 0db90c9d and original-bindings.o 3e53b66d, payload bd112387 \(83886080 bytes; manifest e159639c; 66/66 contract; emmc-block-3-widthhold mapping to gta4l-emmc-block=3 gta4l-emmc-widthhold=1; hold root 327ac826, trust cache 30a72f78 and fixture 00363681\) and BOOT image 7f5455d6 \(85688320 bytes; manifest 33548be7; 18/18 gate; only the ramdisk differs from the ce4d76e8 v5 baseline; header ID adb8232a\), packaged kernel 82ad663d. The widthhold evidence checker recomputes the first-4096-byte window fa169295 \(prefix fa169295a70cc707bcd18d279507df12\) and the last-case 1024-byte slice 5f70bf18 \(prefix 5f70bf18a086007016e948b04aed3b82\) from the final image and checks the one-summary/one-read shape with matching, changed, missing, malformed, truncated and WIDTHHOLDFAIL rejection controls; a real-image run passed and the FAIL rejection returned rc=1. Host results: block 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 344 adapter scenarios with 120 adapter/support and 14 policy mutation controls, core 189/117, probe 196/100, payload builder/package, boot-image, storage-ABI, evidence-count, fixture recipe, inventory/board, packaging and TOML checks pass. No device access, flash, boot, mount or write occurred; widthhold has no hardware result, the N10.16 one-boot budget remains consumed, N10.20 is separately gated and initialization reliability remains \[U\].](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.19-widthhold-candidate-2026-11-11.md)
- `source`: [Records the N10.20 widthhold hardware observation: the N10.19 BOOT image 7f5455d6 \(85688320 bytes; payload bd112387, prelinked kernel 23e3b6d9\) was written to p69 only with the candidate prefix and first 1024 bytes matching, the p69 tail beyond the image unchanged and RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs unchanged; the one approved cold boot returned an owner-transcribed WIDTHHOLD report with cases=4 passed=0 failed=1 reason=5 complete=0, all read/case counters zero \(reads=0 bytes=0 blocks=0 subrequests=0 c18=0 stops=0\), the old schema partition fields zero, switch=4 verified=1 hb=0 ha=2 rb=0 cmd6=1, refs=1 refbytes=4096 refdigest=fa169295 \(independently rehashed from the image first 4096 bytes\) and revert=1 rverified=1 rhb=2 rha=0 rcmd6=1 rstatus=0. Reason 5 is PART; index 0 is an initialized failure field, not an attempted read, and an apparent extra wrapped zero in the WIDTHHOLDFAIL digest is noted but not repaired. Bounded verdict: FAIL at BOOT-partition discovery before any case read, so no sustained 4-bit read was observed. At source 339318b reason 5 covers lookup timeout/duplicates, post-selection size/base rereads, Writable and an exact Content == Apple\_Boot equality; all leave the partition fields zero, so the failing check is unidentifiable from this schema. The Content equality is a source-backed suspect, not a proven cause. The one-boot budget is consumed, the owner returned to TWRP and no retry, reflash, rollback or fresh device collection occurred; the N10.19 image is left as last written and must not be booted again without a new budget. Initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-n10.20-widthhold-observation-2026-11-11.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.20-widthhold-observation-2026-11-11.md)
- `source`: [Records the N10.21 host-only corrective batch from the N10.20 PART failure: the BOOT Content check is structural \(property present as OSString; value, GUID format and length not compared\), because the stock partition scheme publishes a type-GUID string and an attached storage client may install its Content Mask, while actual bytes stay protected by the same-boot reference comparison and final-image host binding. A widthhold-only u32 partdiag field latches the first failing PART category \(bit 0 not-found, 1 duplicate, 2 size, 3 base, 4 Writable, 5 Content\), resets per run, never accumulates, survives report copy/snapshot and is zero on success; partbase/partsize/partro stay zero unless every check passes. The formatter emits partdiag after partro with compile-time u32 and line static asserts. Formatter-exact bounds are 922 bytes for the WIDTHHOLD failure shape \(913 success\), 303 for WIDTHHOLDREAD and 133 for WIDTHHOLDFAIL, with a 2066-byte read-path and 1896-byte fail-path worst report inside the 2560-byte buffer, superseding the pre-N10.21 905/335/227 and 2078/1970 figures. Host results: block 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 351 adapter scenarios with 122 adapter, 3 support and 15 policy mutation controls, including late base/size refusals, Writable true/absent/wrong-typed, Content absent/wrong-typed, friendly-name and synthetic type-GUID acceptance, exact partdiag per refusal, zero partition fields, verified recovery, success partdiag=0 and lost-latch/lost-reset/content-guard/name-equality/Writable-guard/omitted-field controls; core 189/117, probe 196/100 and the evidence-count check pass. scripts/check-gta4l-widthhold-evidence.py requires partdiag=0 on success and rejects nonzero, missing, duplicated, malformed or unknown fields; N10.19 artifacts stay historical. No new candidate image was built, no device access, flash, boot, mount or write occurred, N10.20 stays FAIL, and a source-bound candidate build/ABI audit, protocol and finite budget remain separate gates. Initialization reliability remains \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-n10.21-widthhold-partdiag-candidate-2026-11-11.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.21-widthhold-partdiag-candidate-2026-11-11.md)

- `source`: [Records the N10.23 hardware run: one owner-approved BOOT-only write of the N10.22 image b4d63be7 \(85688320 bytes\) to p69 \(100663296 bytes\) and one owner-triggered boot, after verified backups of BOOT/RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs matching f0940421/14e11370/51fa824c/3a265d58. Post-write full p69 is 7260ad75caf7522b1e6ff7ccb63979b2096dd7d68fd580b1844574e3419a34cb with prefix b4d63be7, tail a28b4ea2 unchanged, last-1KiB a4f065b4 unchanged and the protected partitions unchanged. Report: WIDTHHOLD cases=4 passed=4 failed=0 reason=0 complete=1 match=1 mismatches=0 fails=0 foreign=0 cancel=0 budget=0 partdiag=0, refdigest=obsdigest=417cdef0f05bceafea13119a3b9f1ff5, WIDTHHOLDREAD digest=predigest=5f70bf18a086007016e948b04aed3b82, partbase=1154744320 partsize=100663296 partro=1, verified switch and revert; terminal FAIL valid=0 is the intentional report stop. Post-boot re-measure matched all post-flash values. Budget consumed 1/1; BOOT stays N10.22 and must not be booted again. This is one bounded PASS only: N10.20 stays FAIL, and repeatability, initialization reliability, arbitrary I/O and general 4-bit publication remain \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-n10.23-widthhold-hardware-run-2026-11-12.md and docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.95.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.23-widthhold-hardware-run-2026-11-12.md)

The raw report and verbatim wrapped transcription are owner-held outside the tree.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/check-gta4l-widthhold-evidence.py)

- `source`: [Corrected host-only N10.24 bounded 4-bit published-media read design; the prior publication-hold contract is withdrawn as non-implementation-ready. Records that N10.23 already observed whole-media and BOOT-partition publication and four tagged 2-block reads while at 4-bit followed by a verified revert, so the remaining gap is sustained/general operation and lifecycle, not publication itself. TWRP return demonstrates recovery access, not a physical power cycle, a reset command or a proven width reset; no source-grounded stop/shutdown/quiesce contract exists for leaving the card at 4-bit, so a long-lived hold mode is NO-GO pending that contract, in-flight quiescence, an observed power-cycle reset and owner risk acceptance. Proposed provisional gta4l-emmc-widthpub=1 keeps the N10.23 sequence \(1-bit EXT\_CSD baseline, 8-block/4096-byte 1-bit reference, verified 4-bit switch, inherited LBA-1 read, advertisement and registerService\) and one four-case pass or two passes with one shared total case-loop budget, per-case digest comparison, strict foreign rejection and a verified pre-report 1-bit revert. Start-path timing uses the N10.17 source-derived pre-watchdog bounds 28010 ms success/36030 ms failed switch/38230 ms post-switch failure, not the obsolete 13010 ms two-block estimate; the report thread is 27720 ms only with one shared 3000 ms budget \(a per-pass reset would reach 30720 ms and is excluded\). The decommission audit records that doAsyncReadWrite already sends kIOMessageMediaStateHasChanged\(kIOMediaStateOffline\) after completion and outside gate while the report-thread path only clears online; a report-thread notification delta is an open decision and is not claimed as tested. NO-GO for implementation until the owner accepts the scope, read set, names, decommission behavior and a recomputed formatter/timing bound.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.24-widthpub-design-2026-11-12.md)
- `source`: [Corrected host-only N10.24 widthhold captured repeatability protocol for the unchanged installed N10.22 image b4d63be7 \(85688320 bytes; prelinked kernel 04ae57f6, payload 64914089, full p69 7260ad75, tail a28b4ea2, last-1KiB a4f065b4, RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs 14e11370/51fa824c/3a265d58\). Three additional cold boots are a proposal only, not an approved budget; the owner must select the finite budget, classify each attempt \(cold/warm/unknown\), set a finite observation deadline and approve the read-only device access. Every launch attempt counts, including pre-identification stops and unusable captures. Per-attempt procedure keeps pre/post read-only p69 and protected-partition verification, full-screen capture, labeled normalization of wrapped WIDTHHOLD/WIDTHHOLDREAD lines without value repair, the N10.21 verifier against the exact image, and PASS/pre-identification/widthhold/integrity/insufficient outcome classes. The series stops on the FIRST non-pass of any class \(pre-identification, widthhold, watchdog, integrity, failed recovery, ambiguous capture or owner abort\), with no replacement, automatic retry, reflash, restore, reset/repair, mount, write, host reboot or extra boot. A TWRP start is recovery-access evidence only, not a physical power-cycle or width-reset proof. The series reports planned/attempted/passed/failed/insufficient counts and unused budget, and can describe repeatability but cannot establish a failure rate, initialization reliability, publication readiness or arbitrary I/O.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.24-widthhold-repeatability-protocol-2026-11-12.md)
- `source`: [Host-only N10.24 Track C broader 4-bit coverage disposition. Inventories the delivered N10.11/N10.13/N10.14 six-range 1-bit sweep: A0 4096/4096 bytes 8 blocks, A1 4096/16384 32 blocks, R0 26382336/16384 32 blocks, R1 52699136/8192 16 blocks, R2 78999552/4096 8 blocks, R3 4673536/4096 8 blocks; 53248 bytes, 104 blocks, 13 driver subrequests, max 8 blocks, 63 two-block CMD18 chunks, wid=0, one cold boot PASS. The 4-bit evidence remains only four 2-block reads over the first 4096 bytes \(N10.23\); the block core always chunks at 2 blocks, so the gap is larger driver subrequests, more bytes, multi-subrequest reference/observer attribution, a source-derived 4-bit per-case timing bound and failure-path coverage. An 8-block C2 case is conditionally feasible only after that timing derivation \(the conservative 4500 ms per 2-block chunk gives 41220 ms, above the 30000 ms watchdog\); 16-block, 32-block and full six-range 4-bit parity are NO-GO under the current watchdog and report contract. No C2 design, candidate, hardware run, code change or larger read is authorized.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.24-trackc-4bit-coverage-disposition-2026-11-12.md)
- `source`: [Owner-reported observation after the approved repeatability series on the unchanged installed N10.22 image: the owner executed the boots and reports every boot repeated the N10.23 WIDTHHOLD success shape \(cases=4 passed=4 complete=1, match=1, verified switch and revert, matching WIDTHHOLDREAD digests, no WIDTHHOLDFAIL\) followed by a successful manual TWRP return; no non-pass was reported. No raw capture, normalized lines, verifier log, cold/warm classification, per-attempt record or pre/post p69/RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs hash was supplied, so this is not a captured protocol PASS and does not upgrade the evidence state: N10.23 remains the only captured widthhold hardware PASS, repeatability in the SHA-256-bound sense remains \[U\], and N10.20 remains FAIL. Image hashes are inherited from the N10.23 flash record, not re-measured. No failure rate, initialization reliability, cold/warm correlation, arbitrary I/O, write, mount or publication-readiness claim is made. The agent performed no device access, flash, boot, restore or mount.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-n10.24-widthhold-repeatability-observation-2026-11-12.md)
- `host`: [PASS \(uncommitted N10.24 host implementation on b6dbe59\): implements the owner-accepted bounded gta4l-emmc-widthpub=1 publication-read mode without changing the absent-token legacy, gta4l-emmc-width=4 or gta4l-emmc-widthhold=1 paths. The selector requires exactly one gta4l-emmc-block=3 token and refuses an orphan or duplicate block token, block values other than 3, a malformed, duplicate or non-1 widthpub value and every partobs/partcmp/partread/deblock/breakup/sweep/consume/width/widthhold combination. The mode reuses the widthhold start path \(1-bit EXT\_CSD baseline, 8-block/4096-byte 1-bit reference, verified 4-bit switch, inherited LBA-1 read, 64-block/32768-byte/one-segment advertisement and registerService\) and serves two passes over the four two-block BOOT offsets 0/1024/2048/3072 bytes \(LBAs 2255360/2255362/2255364/2255366\) under one shared 3000 ms case-loop budget, eight tagged cases with exact counts, clean stops and per-case digest equality, strict foreign rejection and a verified pre-report 1-bit revert. The pub marker is set only after whole-media and BOOT-partition discovery while at 4-bit and records publication, not qualification success; a discovery failure leaves pub=0. widthpub adds the report-thread offline notification delta through a one-shot latch shared with the async read path, sends kIOMessageMediaStateHasChanged\(kIOMediaStateOffline\) exactly once per terminal failure \(from the report thread after gate/client release and quiescence or from the failing completion through the same latch\), never on success, and leaves the 1-bit and widthhold paths unchanged. Formatter bounds are 936/302/132 bytes with a 2079-byte worst report inside 2560; the report thread remains 27720 ms under the 30000 ms watchdog and the pre-watchdog path remains conditional at 28010/36030/38230 ms. Host results: block 48 core scenarios/38 mutation controls and 370 adapter scenarios \(371 PASS lines\) including the widthpub full pass, publication, two-pass budget, read/short/match/foreign/cancel/gate/revert/baseline/switch failures, media-absent and selector refusals, with single-pass, window-collapse, pub-cleared, pub-before-discovery, offline-missing, offline-latch, value-accepted, duplicate-accepted and widthhold-conflict-accepted mutation controls; core 189/117, probe 196/100, documented evidence counts and scripts/check-gta4l-widthpub-evidence.py --self-test pass. The checker recomputes both window digests and the last-case slice from the final image and rejects changed content, missing/duplicate/extra fields or lines, nonzero mismatch/foreign/cancel/budget and a WIDTHHOLD-labelled summary. No candidate build, ABI/packaging audit, device access, flash, boot, mount or write occurred; N10.23 remains the only captured widthhold hardware PASS, N10.20 remains FAIL, and general 4-bit publication, sustained/arbitrary I/O, repeatability and initialization reliability remain \[U\]. Findings: docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.98 and docs/emmc-n10.24-widthpub-design-2026-11-12.md.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/test-gta4l-emmc-block.py)

Revision: `29a4fcd28585d1760dc23b173282126ceae67e81`.

- `source`: [Records the S1 speed hardware run and verdict: one owner boot of the 805a5b66 candidate \(source 5d82708, kernel a2fdd945, archive 03e581e5, payload d8967f74, 85688320 bytes\) after a BOOT-only write to p69 \(100663296 bytes\) whose preflight matched the protected baseline \(full 7e209188, partition-end last 1 KiB d737414c, RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs 14e11370/51fa824c/3a265d58\) and whose postflight read 5342e715 with the candidate prefix and unchanged tail, last 1 KiB and protected partitions. The report reached the intentional terminal stop at Gta4lEmmcBlock.cpp:9070 with rc=0 stop=0 mode=3 wid=0 tim=0 clk=400000 after the verified revert; SPEED hs=1 hsrb=0 hsa=1 rcg=0000020b hispd=00 wtry=8 wfall=0 wroll=0 wid=8 verified=1 hb=00 ha=20 cmd6=1 base=1 bytes1=4096 ticks1=55912 hz=19200000 dig1=19b66b41401e75a6e561f11ecc8a5071 bytes2=4096 ticks2=46348 dig2=19b66b41401e75a6e561f11ecc8a5071 req=8 fails=0 cancel=0 budget=0 partial=0 pub=1 partdiag=0 revert=1 rverified=1 rhb=20 rha=00 rcmd6=2 rstatus=0 hsrevert=1 hsverified=1 rhsb=1 rhs=0 rclk=400000 limit=64 seg=1 cap=61071360; MEDIA whole=1 base=0 GUID\_partition\_scheme writable=0; SNAP locked=1; FAIL valid=0. The two passes are 2.912 and 2.414 ms per 4 KiB \(1.41 and 1.70 MB/s\); the wire time at 8-bit / 50 MHz is 81.9 us, so about 96.6 percent is per-request overhead and the full BOOT region would need about 50 s. A read-only post-boot re-measure matched every post-flash value. The verdict qualifies the first 4 KiB of BOOT on one boot only; it does not qualify sustained I/O, the full region, writes, DMA, a mount, repeatability, initialization reliability, the electrical margin, pad or DLL state, or lifetime fast mode. The cold/warm class and boot ordinal were not recorded, and the transcript needed two owner-confirmed character fixes \(hswerified to hsverified, Gta41 to Gta4l\) that the normalizer did not apply.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-s1-speed-design-2026-11-12.md)

Read-only post-boot hashing matched the post-flash record \(full 5342e715, prefix 805a5b66, tail ad7facb2, partition-end last 1 KiB d737414c, RECOVERY 14e11370, EFS 51fa824c, sec\_efs 3a265d58, battery 100 percent\). The raw transcript, join log and correction note stay outside the tracked tree.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/scripts/check-gta4l-speed-evidence.py)

- `source`: [Records the S2 bulk hardware run 1 and verdict: one owner boot of candidate c6e23141 \(source working tree after de7d0d1, kernel 053b5c57, archive 1613fb52, payload 182c3ad4, 85688320 bytes\) after a BOOT-only write to p69 \(100663296 bytes\) whose preflight matched the protected baseline \(full 5342e715, prefix 805a5b66, tail a28b4ea2, RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs 14e11370/51fa824c/3a265d58\) and whose postflight read 513a0e38 with the candidate prefix c6e23141 and unchanged tail and protected partitions. The diagnostic streamed 279 of 2615 64-block requests \(9142272 of 85688320 bytes\) before the controlled 4000 ms stream-budget exit; BULK recorded chunks=279 bytes=9142272 ticks=77071679 hz=19200000 seed=47544134 windows=80 wpass=0 digest=5710fc38b12378dc5786da8d22b4a173, BULKFAIL stage=6 status=0 cancel=0 budget=1, and SPEEDIO req=279 blk=17856 inh=13447 cmd=18710 dat=63288896 stop=139839 core=63510393 bus=63798706 io=63884420. The prefix digest matches the host SHA-256 of the first 9142272 image bytes; the width/HS revert is verified \(rverified=1 hsverified=1 rclk=400000\) and cfail=0 sfail=0 fails=0. The data phase is 184.6 us per 512-byte block, 18.0x the 8-bit / 50 MHz wire time, while inhibit, command and stop total about 9 ms \(under 1 percent\) and hashing is about 0.69 s for 9142272 bytes; the full region projects to about 37 s and does not fit the 30000 ms watchdog. This refutes the S1 section 9 attribution of 96.6 percent to per-request overhead and leaves the effective card clock/width versus MMIO drain cost unresolved. The verdict is INCOMPLETE: a verified prefix on one boot, not the full region, the 80 windows, sustained I/O, a rate claim, writes, DMA, a mount, repeatability or initialization reliability. See docs/emmc-driver.md section 5.3.106.](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-b8-overhead-decision-2026-11-13.md)

The SMMU line reports st=pre/read/done, scr0, id0, nsmrg, the matched index/value, s2cr, cb and dec=bypass/fault/translate/map-fail. CLIENTPD=1, a bypass S2CR, or no match with USFCFG=0 allow DMA; a fault S2CR or no match with USFCFG=1 and a translate S2CR are skip reasons; a map failure reports map-fail and leaves PIO unchanged. - `source`: [Records the B10.11 owner boot of fcb1603b: BOOT-only p69 write verified \(full cf5fcc51, prefix fcb1603b, tail a28b4ea2, RECOVERY/EFS/sec\_efs 14e11370/51fa824c/3a265d58 unchanged\) and one owner boot; device returned to TWRP. The FORMAT fix worked \(dec=cb-off, fsr=00000400, pfsr=pgfsr=0\) and DMA ran for the first time \(adma2-32\), but failed immediately: cmp=0/4, stage=4 \(read\), ast=00000001 \(ST\_FDS\), aaddr=875c3018 \(0x18 past a one-entry 8-byte table\), desc=4294967295, dat=1219 ticks \(\~63 us\), fails=1, pfsr=0, BULKFAIL stage=12 with chunks=0, verified 1-bit/400 kHz revert \(revert=1 rverified=1 rclk=400000 hsrevert=1\), FAIL valid=0. Leading cause: the DMA path performs no CPU cache maintenance for the write-back descriptor table or bounce buffer on a non-coherent controller; H2-H4 \(stale buffer lines, 64-bit mode, hidden stage-2\) remain possible because the boot did not capture the SDHCI error/normal/present values, table/buffer physicals, entry count, HC1/HC2 or ADMA address readback. Safety: garbage descriptors may have used arbitrary RAM as write targets for \~63 us; card writes are disabled and RAM is lost at reset. B10.12 adds performOperation Store/Flush maintenance plus a failure-only DMADIAG capture and a non-coherent host model. Do not boot fcb1603b again. Evidence: out/gta4l/ram-root-emmc-b10.11-cb-format-evidence \(flash result, normalized field transcription and source limitation; raw paste not retained\).](https://github.com/gta4lunix/UniXNU-gta4l/blob/135a3ad04178a9a2d4c1d74aa54ac8b170597aed/docs/emmc-driver.md)
